"""
src/run_experiments.py
======================
Main experiment runner for the scripted (CPU-only) study.

Usage:
    python -m src.run_experiments --conditions A B C D --seeds 20 --rounds 200 --tag scripted_main

Protocol:
1. Generates trajectories for specified conditions and seeds.
2. Runs detectors on raw trajectory data (no ground truth exposed).
3. Calibrates decision thresholds on validation seeds (seeds 0-4 of Condition A) to target 5% FPR.
4. Evaluates ranking metrics (Top-1 accuracy, MRR) and calibrated FPR.
5. Aggregates results per condition using Wilson score intervals for Top-1 accuracy
   and non-parametric bootstrap CIs for continuous scores.
6. Saves results to results/metrics/ and results/tables/.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import json
import csv
import datetime
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

# Resolve project root
PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent
DATA_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "data"
RESULTS_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "results"
RESULTS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
(RESULTS_DIR / "metrics").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
(RESULTS_DIR / "tables").mkdir(exist_ok=True)

sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))

from src.trajectories import run_trajectory, save_trajectory, load_trajectory, load_ground_truth
from src.detectors import run_detectors, ALL_PAIRS
from src.metrics import (
    evaluate_trajectory,
    aggregate_metrics,
    chance_top1_accuracy,
    calibrate_thresholds_from_validation,
)
from src.environment import N_AGENTS

COORDINATED_PAIR = (0, 1)
CHANCE_TOP1 = chance_top1_accuracy()


def run_single_trajectory(
    condition: str,
    seed: int,
    n_rounds: int,
    alpha: float = 1.0,
    noise: float = 0.1,
    overwrite: bool = True,
) -> dict:
    """Generate trajectory and run detectors (without ground truth)."""
    traj = run_trajectory(
        condition=condition,
        seed=seed,
        n_rounds=n_rounds,
        alpha=alpha,
        coordinated_pair=COORDINATED_PAIR,
        noise=noise,
    )
    
    # Save to disk and reload (validates JSONL pipeline)
    traj_path, gt_path = save_trajectory(traj, DATA_DIR, overwrite=overwrite)
    meta, rounds = load_trajectory(traj_path)
    gt = load_ground_truth(gt_path)
    
    true_pair = tuple(gt["coordinating_pair"]) if gt["coordinating_pair"] else None
    
    # Run detectors — they do NOT receive the ground truth
    detector_results = run_detectors(rounds, threshold=0.0)
    
    return {
        "run_id": meta["run_id"],
        "condition": condition,
        "seed": seed,
        "n_rounds": n_rounds,
        "alpha": alpha if condition == "D" else None,
        "true_pair": true_pair,
        "detector_results": detector_results,
    }


def run_experiment(
    conditions: list[str],
    seeds: list[int],
    n_rounds: int,
    alphas: list[float] = None,
    noise: float = 0.1,
    run_tag: str = "default",
    overwrite: bool = True,
    val_seed_cutoff: int = 5,
    target_fpr: float = 0.05,
    fixed_thresholds: Optional[dict[str, float]] = None,
) -> dict:
    """Run full experiment across conditions and seeds with validation calibration or fixed thresholds."""
    if alphas is None:
        alphas = [0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0]
    
    timestamp = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()
    raw_runs: list[dict] = []
    
    print(f"--- Running Trajectories: {len(conditions)} conditions, {len(seeds)} seeds, {n_rounds} rounds ---")
    
    for condition in conditions:
        print(f"\n=== Condition {condition} ===")
        if condition == "D":
            for alpha in alphas:
                for seed in seeds:
                    print(f"  seed={seed:04d}, alpha={alpha:.2f}", end=" ... ")
                    run_dict = run_single_trajectory(
                        condition=condition,
                        seed=seed,
                        n_rounds=n_rounds,
                        alpha=alpha,
                        noise=noise,
                        overwrite=overwrite,
                    )
                    raw_runs.append(run_dict)
                    pni_top = run_dict["detector_results"]["pni"][0]["pair"]
                    print(f"pni_top_pair={pni_top}")
        else:
            for seed in seeds:
                print(f"  seed={seed:04d}", end=" ... ")
                run_dict = run_single_trajectory(
                    condition=condition,
                    seed=seed,
                    n_rounds=n_rounds,
                    noise=noise,
                    overwrite=overwrite,
                )
                raw_runs.append(run_dict)
                pni_top = run_dict["detector_results"]["pni"][0]["pair"]
                print(f"pni_top_pair={pni_top}")
                
    # Decision Threshold Determination
    if fixed_thresholds is not None:
        if not ("pni" in fixed_thresholds and "marginal_mi" in fixed_thresholds):
            raise ValueError(f"fixed_thresholds must contain 'pni' and 'marginal_mi', got {fixed_thresholds}")
        calibrated_thresholds = {k: float(v) for k, v in fixed_thresholds.items()}
        if "cmi" not in calibrated_thresholds:
            calibrated_thresholds["cmi"] = calibrated_thresholds.get("marginal_mi", 0.0)
        print(f"\nUsing Fixed Decision Thresholds:")
        for det, th in calibrated_thresholds.items():
            print(f"  {det}: {th:.6f} bits")
    else:
        # Threshold Calibration on Validation Seeds (Condition A, seeds < val_seed_cutoff)
        val_records = [
            r for r in raw_runs
            if r["condition"] == "A" and r["seed"] < val_seed_cutoff
        ]
        if not val_records:
            raise ValueError(
                "Condition A validation records (seeds < val_seed_cutoff) were not found, "
                "and no fixed_thresholds were provided! Cannot determine decision thresholds."
            )
        calibrated_thresholds = calibrate_thresholds_from_validation(val_records, target_fpr=target_fpr)
        print(f"\nCalibrated Thresholds (target FPR={target_fpr:.2f} on Cond A val seeds 0-{val_seed_cutoff-1}):")
        for det, th in calibrated_thresholds.items():
            print(f"  {det}: {th:.6f} bits")
        
    # Evaluate Metrics for all runs using calibrated thresholds
    evaluated_results: list[dict] = []
    for r in raw_runs:
        m = evaluate_trajectory(
            detector_results=r["detector_results"],
            true_pair=r["true_pair"],
            condition=r["condition"],
            thresholds=calibrated_thresholds,
        )
        m["run_id"] = r["run_id"]
        m["seed"] = r["seed"]
        m["n_rounds"] = r["n_rounds"]
        m["alpha"] = r["alpha"]
        m["is_val_seed"] = bool(r["condition"] == "A" and r["seed"] < val_seed_cutoff)
        
        # Record top ranked pairs and scores
        for det_name, ranked in r["detector_results"].items():
            if ranked and "pair" in ranked[0]:
                m[f"{det_name}_top_ranked_pair"] = list(ranked[0]["pair"])
                m[f"{det_name}_top_score"] = float(ranked[0]["score"])
                
        evaluated_results.append(m)
        
    # Aggregate metrics per condition
    # CRITICAL: Condition A test performance MUST exclude calibration seeds (seeds < val_seed_cutoff)
    agg_by_condition = {}
    for condition in conditions:
        if condition == "D":
            for alpha in alphas:
                key = f"D_alpha{alpha:.2f}"
                valid = [r for r in evaluated_results
                         if r.get("condition") == "D"
                         and abs((r.get("alpha") or 0) - alpha) < 1e-9]
                agg_by_condition[key] = aggregate_metrics(valid)
        elif condition == "A":
            # Strictly held-out test evaluation: seeds val_seed_cutoff to n_seeds-1 (n=15)
            test_runs = [r for r in evaluated_results if r.get("condition") == "A" and not r.get("is_val_seed")]
            agg_by_condition["A"] = aggregate_metrics(test_runs)
            # Separately record calibration performance for complete transparency
            cal_runs = [r for r in evaluated_results if r.get("condition") == "A" and r.get("is_val_seed")]
            if cal_runs:
                agg_by_condition["A_calibration"] = aggregate_metrics(cal_runs)
        else:
            valid = [r for r in evaluated_results if r.get("condition") == condition]
            agg_by_condition[condition] = aggregate_metrics(valid)
            
    output = {
        "run_tag": run_tag,
        "timestamp": timestamp,
        "config": {
            "conditions": conditions,
            "n_seeds": len(seeds),
            "seeds": seeds,
            "n_rounds": n_rounds,
            "alphas": alphas if "D" in conditions else None,
            "noise": noise,
            "coordinated_pair": list(COORDINATED_PAIR),
            "n_agents": N_AGENTS,
            "chance_top1": CHANCE_TOP1,
            "val_seed_cutoff": val_seed_cutoff,
            "calibrated_thresholds": calibrated_thresholds,
            "threshold_mode": "fixed" if fixed_thresholds is not None else "calibrated_from_cond_a",
        },
        "per_run_results": evaluated_results,
        "aggregated": agg_by_condition,
    }
    
    return output


def save_results(output: dict, results_dir: Path, run_tag: str) -> dict:
    """Save results to JSON and CSV."""
    ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    (results_dir / "metrics").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (results_dir / "tables").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (results_dir / "figures").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
    # Save full timestamped JSON
    json_path = results_dir / "metrics" / f"results_{run_tag}_{ts}.json"
    with open(json_path, "w") as f:
        json.dump(output, f, indent=2, default=_json_serializer)
    print(f"Saved: {json_path}")
    
    # Save canonical final_metrics.json
    final_json = results_dir / "metrics" / "final_metrics.json"
    with open(final_json, "w") as f:
        json.dump(output, f, indent=2, default=_json_serializer)
    print(f"Saved: {final_json}")
    
    # Save summary CSV
    SKIP_METRICS = {"seed", "n_rounds", "alpha", "has_true_pair", "is_val_seed"}
    rows = []
    for cond_key, agg in output["aggregated"].items():
        row = {"condition": cond_key}
        for metric, stats in agg.items():
            if metric in SKIP_METRICS:
                continue
            if isinstance(stats, dict):
                row[f"{metric}_mean"] = stats.get("mean", np.nan)
                row[f"{metric}_ci_low"] = stats.get("ci_low", np.nan)
                row[f"{metric}_ci_high"] = stats.get("ci_high", np.nan)
                row[f"{metric}_n"] = stats.get("n", 0)
        rows.append(row)
        
    csv_path = results_dir / "tables" / f"summary_{run_tag}_{ts}.csv"
    if rows:
        with open(csv_path, "w", newline="") as f:
            writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=list(rows[0].keys()))
            writer.writeheader()
            writer.writerows(rows)
        print(f"Saved: {csv_path}")
        
        # Save canonical summary.csv
        canon_csv = results_dir / "tables" / "summary.csv"
        with open(canon_csv, "w", newline="") as f:
            writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=list(rows[0].keys()))
            writer.writeheader()
            writer.writerows(rows)
        print(f"Saved: {canon_csv}")
        
    return {"json": str(json_path), "csv": str(csv_path)}


def _json_serializer(obj):
    if isinstance(obj, (np.integer, np.int64)):
        return int(obj)
    if isinstance(obj, (np.floating, np.float64)):
        return float(obj)
    if isinstance(obj, np.ndarray):
        return obj.tolist()
    if isinstance(obj, (set, frozenset)):
        return list(obj)
    raise TypeError(f"Object of type {type(obj)} is not JSON serializable")


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run collusion detection experiments.")
    parser.add_argument("--conditions", nargs="+", default=["A", "B", "C", "D"], help="Conditions to run.")
    parser.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, default=20, help="Number of seeds (0 to seeds-1).")
    parser.add_argument("--rounds", type=int, default=200, help="Number of rounds per trajectory.")
    parser.add_argument("--alphas", nargs="+", type=float, default=[0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0], help="Alpha values for condition D.")
    parser.add_argument("--tag", default="scripted_main", help="Run identifier tag.")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    output = run_experiment(
        conditions=args.conditions,
        seeds=list(range(args.seeds)),
        n_rounds=args.rounds,
        alphas=args.alphas,
        run_tag=args.tag,
        overwrite=True,
    )
    
    save_results(output, RESULTS_DIR, run_tag=args.tag)
    print("\nExperiment complete.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""
analyze_results.py
==================
Post-hoc analysis and plotting from saved experiment results.

Usage:
    python -m src.analyze_results
    python -m src.analyze_results --results-dir results/metrics --output-dir results/figures
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")  # headless
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import matplotlib.patches as mpatches

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))

FIGURES_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "results" / "figures"
TABLES_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "results" / "tables"
FIGURES_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
TABLES_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

CHANCE_TOP1 = 1.0 / 6.0  # 6 unordered pairs
FIGSIZE = (8, 5)
DPI = 150


def load_results(metrics_dir: Path) -> dict:
    """Load the most recent final_metrics.json."""
    final = metrics_dir / "final_metrics.json"
    if not final.exists():
        # Try most recent
        candidates = sorted(metrics_dir.glob("results_*.json"), reverse=True)
        if not candidates:
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"No results found in {metrics_dir}")
        final = candidates[0]
    with open(final) as f:
        return json.load(f)


def _safe_stat(agg: dict, cond_key: str, metric: str, stat: str = "mean") -> float:
    """Safely extract a stat from aggregated results."""
    try:
        return agg[cond_key][metric][stat]
    except (KeyError, TypeError):
        return np.nan


def plot_top1_comparison(output: dict, figures_dir: Path):
    """Figure 1: Top-1 accuracy comparison across conditions."""
    agg = output["aggregated"]
    chance = output["config"]["chance_top1"]
    
    conditions = ["C"]  # conditions with ground truth
    alphas = output["config"].get("alphas") or []
    if alphas:
        conditions += [f"D_alpha{a:.2f}" for a in alphas if f"D_alpha{a:.2f}" in agg]
    
    pni_means = [_safe_stat(agg, c, "pni_top1") for c in conditions]
    pni_lo = [_safe_stat(agg, c, "pni_top1", "ci_low") for c in conditions]
    pni_hi = [_safe_stat(agg, c, "pni_top1", "ci_high") for c in conditions]
    mi_means = [_safe_stat(agg, c, "marginal_mi_top1") for c in conditions]
    mi_lo = [_safe_stat(agg, c, "marginal_mi_top1", "ci_low") for c in conditions]
    mi_hi = [_safe_stat(agg, c, "marginal_mi_top1", "ci_high") for c in conditions]
    
    x = np.arange(len(conditions))
    width = 0.35
    
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=FIGSIZE)
    
    bars1 = ax.bar(x - width/2, pni_means, width, label="PNI (Bonjour et al.)",
                   color="#2196F3", alpha=0.85)
    bars2 = ax.bar(x + width/2, mi_means, width, label="Marginal MI (baseline)",
                   color="#FF9800", alpha=0.85)
    
    # Error bars for CI
    for i, (lo, hi) in enumerate(zip(pni_lo, pni_hi)):
        if not (np.isnan(lo) or np.isnan(hi)):
            mid = pni_means[i]
            ax.errorbar(x[i] - width/2, mid, yerr=[[mid - lo], [hi - mid]],
                        fmt="none", color="black", capsize=4, lw=1.5)
    for i, (lo, hi) in enumerate(zip(mi_lo, mi_hi)):
        if not (np.isnan(lo) or np.isnan(hi)):
            mid = mi_means[i]
            ax.errorbar(x[i] + width/2, mid, yerr=[[mid - lo], [hi - mid]],
                        fmt="none", color="black", capsize=4, lw=1.5)
    
    ax.axhline(chance, color="red", linestyle="--", linewidth=1.5,
               label=f"Chance ({chance:.3f})")
    
    labels = ["Cond C\n(full coord.)"]
    if alphas:
        labels += [f"Cond D\nα={a:.2f}" for a in alphas if f"D_alpha{a:.2f}" in agg]
    
    ax.set_xticks(x)
    ax.set_xticklabels(labels)
    ax.set_ylim(0, 1.1)
    ax.set_ylabel("Top-1 Accuracy (fraction of seeds)")
    ax.set_title("Detector Top-1 Accuracy: Identifying the Coordinating Pair\n"
                 f"(n={output['config']['n_seeds']} seeds, {output['config']['n_rounds']} rounds, 95% CI)")
    ax.legend(loc="upper right")
    ax.grid(axis="y", alpha=0.3)
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    path = figures_dir / "fig1_top1_accuracy.png"
    plt.savefig(path, dpi=DPI)
    plt.close()
    print(f"Saved: {path}")
    return path


def plot_fpr_comparison(output: dict, figures_dir: Path):
    """Figure 2: False positive rates for conditions A, B (negative controls)."""
    agg = output["aggregated"]
    conditions_shown = []
    labels_shown = []
    
    if "A" in agg:
        conditions_shown.append("A")
        labels_shown.append("Cond A (Test)\n(indep., n=15)")
    if "B" in agg:
        conditions_shown.append("B")
        labels_shown.append("Cond B\n(context, n=20)")
    
    if not conditions_shown:
        print("No negative control conditions found for FPR plot.")
        return None
    
    pni_fpr = [_safe_stat(agg, c, "pni_fpr") for c in conditions_shown]
    pni_fpr_lo = [_safe_stat(agg, c, "pni_fpr", "ci_low") for c in conditions_shown]
    pni_fpr_hi = [_safe_stat(agg, c, "pni_fpr", "ci_high") for c in conditions_shown]
    cmi_fpr = [_safe_stat(agg, c, "cmi_fpr") for c in conditions_shown]
    cmi_fpr_lo = [_safe_stat(agg, c, "cmi_fpr", "ci_low") for c in conditions_shown]
    cmi_fpr_hi = [_safe_stat(agg, c, "cmi_fpr", "ci_high") for c in conditions_shown]
    mi_fpr = [_safe_stat(agg, c, "marginal_mi_fpr") for c in conditions_shown]
    mi_fpr_lo = [_safe_stat(agg, c, "marginal_mi_fpr", "ci_low") for c in conditions_shown]
    mi_fpr_hi = [_safe_stat(agg, c, "marginal_mi_fpr", "ci_high") for c in conditions_shown]
    
    x = np.arange(len(conditions_shown))
    width = 0.25
    
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=FIGSIZE)
    
    ax.bar(x - width, pni_fpr, width, label="PNI (conditioned + rival sub)", color="#2196F3", alpha=0.85)
    ax.bar(x, cmi_fpr, width, label="Conditional MI (state-conditioned)", color="#9C27B0", alpha=0.85)
    ax.bar(x + width, mi_fpr, width, label="Marginal MI (unconditioned)", color="#FF9800", alpha=0.85)
    
    for i, (lo, hi) in enumerate(zip(pni_fpr_lo, pni_fpr_hi)):
        if not (np.isnan(lo) or np.isnan(hi)):
            mid = pni_fpr[i]
            ax.errorbar(x[i] - width, mid, yerr=[[mid - lo], [hi - mid]],
                        fmt="none", color="black", capsize=4, lw=1.5)
    for i, (lo, hi) in enumerate(zip(cmi_fpr_lo, cmi_fpr_hi)):
        if not (np.isnan(lo) or np.isnan(hi)):
            mid = cmi_fpr[i]
            ax.errorbar(x[i], mid, yerr=[[mid - lo], [hi - mid]],
                        fmt="none", color="black", capsize=4, lw=1.5)
    for i, (lo, hi) in enumerate(zip(mi_fpr_lo, mi_fpr_hi)):
        if not (np.isnan(lo) or np.isnan(hi)):
            mid = mi_fpr[i]
            ax.errorbar(x[i] + width, mid, yerr=[[mid - lo], [hi - mid]],
                        fmt="none", color="black", capsize=4, lw=1.5)
    
    ax.set_xticks(x)
    ax.set_xticklabels(labels_shown)
    ax.set_ylim(0, 1.05)
    cal_th = output.get("config", {}).get("calibrated_thresholds", {})
    if cal_th and "pni" in cal_th and "marginal_mi" in cal_th:
        th_p = cal_th.get('pni', 0.0)
        th_c = cal_th.get('cmi', 0.0)
        th_m = cal_th.get('marginal_mi', 0.0)
        th_str = f"calibrated τ (PNI={th_p:.4f}, CMI={th_c:.4f}, MI={th_m:.4f})"
    else:
        th_str = "threshold=0.0"
    ax.set_title("False Positive Rates on Negative Controls\n"
                 f"({th_str}, 95% CI)")
    ax.legend()
    ax.grid(axis="y", alpha=0.3)
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    path = figures_dir / "fig2_false_positive_rates.png"
    plt.savefig(path, dpi=DPI)
    plt.close()
    print(f"Saved: {path}")
    return path


def plot_coordination_strength_sweep(output: dict, figures_dir: Path):
    """Figure 3: Detection quality vs coordination strength (Condition D)."""
    agg = output["aggregated"]
    alphas = output["config"].get("alphas") or []
    
    d_keys = [f"D_alpha{a:.2f}" for a in alphas if f"D_alpha{a:.2f}" in agg]
    if not d_keys:
        print("No Condition D results for coordination strength plot.")
        return None
    
    alpha_vals = [float(k.split("alpha")[1]) for k in d_keys]
    
    pni_top1 = [_safe_stat(agg, k, "pni_top1") for k in d_keys]
    pni_top1_lo = [_safe_stat(agg, k, "pni_top1", "ci_low") for k in d_keys]
    pni_top1_hi = [_safe_stat(agg, k, "pni_top1", "ci_high") for k in d_keys]
    mi_top1 = [_safe_stat(agg, k, "marginal_mi_top1") for k in d_keys]
    
    chance = output["config"]["chance_top1"]
    
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=FIGSIZE)
    
    ax.plot(alpha_vals, pni_top1, "o-", color="#2196F3", label="PNI detector", linewidth=2)
    ax.fill_between(alpha_vals, pni_top1_lo, pni_top1_hi, color="#2196F3", alpha=0.2,
                    label="PNI 95% CI")
    ax.plot(alpha_vals, mi_top1, "s--", color="#FF9800", label="Marginal MI", linewidth=2)
    ax.axhline(chance, color="red", linestyle=":", linewidth=1.5,
               label=f"Chance ({chance:.3f})")
    
    ax.set_xlabel("Coordination Strength α\n(probability of following coordination policy)")
    ax.set_ylabel("Top-1 Accuracy")
    ax.set_ylim(0, 1.1)
    ax.set_xlim(-0.05, 1.05)
    ax.set_title("Detection Accuracy vs Coordination Strength\n"
                 f"(Condition D, n={output['config']['n_seeds']} seeds, {output['config']['n_rounds']} rounds)")
    ax.legend()
    ax.grid(alpha=0.3)
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    path = figures_dir / "fig3_coordination_strength.png"
    plt.savefig(path, dpi=DPI)
    plt.close()
    print(f"Saved: {path}")
    return path


def plot_score_distributions(output: dict, figures_dir: Path):
    """Figure 4: Distribution of true-pair scores per condition."""
    per_run = output.get("per_run_results", [])
    if not per_run:
        return None
    
    conditions_to_plot = {}
    for r in per_run:
        if "error" in r:
            continue
        cond = r.get("condition", "?")
        alpha = r.get("alpha")
        if cond == "D" and alpha is not None:
            key = f"D_α={alpha:.2f}"
        else:
            key = cond
        
        pni_score = r.get("pni_true_score")
        if pni_score is not None and not np.isnan(pni_score):
            conditions_to_plot.setdefault(key, []).append(pni_score)
    
    if not conditions_to_plot:
        return None
    
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(max(6, len(conditions_to_plot) * 1.2), 5))
    
    sorted_keys = sorted(conditions_to_plot.keys())
    data = [conditions_to_plot[k] for k in sorted_keys]
    
    bp = ax.boxplot(data, tick_labels=sorted_keys, patch_artist=True, notch=False)
    colors = plt.cm.Blues(np.linspace(0.3, 0.9, len(data)))
    for patch, color in zip(bp["boxes"], colors):
        patch.set_facecolor(color)
    
    ax.axhline(0, color="red", linestyle="--", linewidth=1.0, label="Threshold=0")
    ax.set_ylabel("PNI Score of True Pair\n(min of Γ(i→j), Γ(j→i))")
    ax.set_title("Distribution of PNI Score for True Coordinating Pair\n"
                 "(positive score → detector ranks this pair above competitors)")
    ax.grid(axis="y", alpha=0.3)
    ax.legend()
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    path = figures_dir / "fig4_score_distributions.png"
    plt.savefig(path, dpi=DPI)
    plt.close()
    print(f"Saved: {path}")
    return path


def write_summary_csv(output: dict, tables_dir: Path):
    """Write a clean summary CSV of aggregated metrics."""
    import csv
    
    agg = output["aggregated"]
    rows = []
    
    for cond_key, metrics in sorted(agg.items()):
        row = {"condition": cond_key}
        SKIP_METRICS = {"seed", "n_rounds", "alpha", "has_true_pair"}
        for metric_name, stats in sorted(metrics.items()):
            if metric_name in SKIP_METRICS:
                continue
            if isinstance(stats, dict):
                row[f"{metric_name}_mean"] = _fmt(stats.get("mean"))
                row[f"{metric_name}_ci_low"] = _fmt(stats.get("ci_low"))
                row[f"{metric_name}_ci_high"] = _fmt(stats.get("ci_high"))
                row[f"{metric_name}_n"] = stats.get("n", "")
        rows.append(row)
    
    if not rows:
        return None
    
    path = tables_dir / "summary.csv"
    with open(path, "w", newline="") as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=list(rows[0].keys()))
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)
    print(f"Saved: {path}")
    return path


def _fmt(v):
    if v is None or (isinstance(v, float) and np.isnan(v)):
        return ""
    return f"{v:.4f}"


def write_results_interpretation(output: dict, docs_dir: Path):
    """Write a machine-generated results interpretation."""
    agg = output["aggregated"]
    config = output["config"]
    
    chance = config["chance_top1"]
    
    lines = [
        "# Results Interpretation",
        "",
        f"**Experiment:** {config['n_seeds']} seeds × {config['n_rounds']} rounds per trajectory",
        f"**Chance top-1 accuracy:** {chance:.3f} (1/6 pairs)",
        f"**Coordinated pair:** agents {config['coordinated_pair']}",
        "",
        "## Condition A (Independent Baseline)",
    ]
    
    if "A" in agg:
        pni_fpr_a = _safe_stat(agg, "A", "pni_fpr")
        mi_fpr_a = _safe_stat(agg, "A", "marginal_mi_fpr")
        lines += [
            f"- PNI false positive rate: {pni_fpr_a:.3f}",
            f"- Marginal MI false positive rate: {mi_fpr_a:.3f}",
            "- Interpretation: Under fully independent behavior, a well-calibrated",
            "  detector should have a low false positive rate.",
        ]
    
    lines += ["", "## Condition B (Shared-Context Negative Control)"]
    
    if "B" in agg:
        pni_fpr_b = _safe_stat(agg, "B", "pni_fpr")
        mi_fpr_b = _safe_stat(agg, "B", "marginal_mi_fpr")
        lines += [
            f"- PNI false positive rate: {pni_fpr_b:.3f}",
            f"- Marginal MI false positive rate: {mi_fpr_b:.3f}",
            "- H2 test: Marginal MI FPR > PNI FPR would support H2 (shared context",
            "  confounds unconditional detectors but not conditional ones).",
        ]
    
    lines += ["", "## Condition C (Known-Coordination Positive Control)"]
    
    if "C" in agg:
        pni_top1 = _safe_stat(agg, "C", "pni_top1")
        pni_top1_lo = _safe_stat(agg, "C", "pni_top1", "ci_low")
        pni_top1_hi = _safe_stat(agg, "C", "pni_top1", "ci_high")
        mi_top1 = _safe_stat(agg, "C", "marginal_mi_top1")
        lines += [
            f"- PNI top-1 accuracy: {pni_top1:.3f} (95% CI: [{pni_top1_lo:.3f}, {pni_top1_hi:.3f}])",
            f"- Marginal MI top-1 accuracy: {mi_top1:.3f}",
            f"- Chance baseline: {chance:.3f}",
            f"- H1 support: {'Supported' if pni_top1_lo > chance else 'Not clearly supported'}",
            "  (H1 requires lower CI bound above chance).",
        ]
    
    lines += ["", "## Key Limitations"]
    lines += [
        "- Small coordinating pair; only one pair tested.",
        "- RPS is simpler than real economic games.",
        "- Results apply to scripted agents with controlled coordination mechanisms.",
        "- The coordination mechanism (shared deterministic hash function) is artificial.",
        "- No LLM agents were tested in this run (scripted experiment only).",
        "- Short trajectories may underestimate detection performance.",
    ]
    
    docs_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path = docs_dir / "results_interpretation.md"
    with open(path, "w") as f:
        f.write("\n".join(lines))
    print(f"Saved: {path}")
    return path


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Analyze experiment results and generate figures")
    parser.add_argument("--results-dir", type=Path, default=PROJECT_ROOT / "results" / "metrics")
    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, default=FIGURES_DIR)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    print(f"Loading results from: {args.results_dir}")
    output = load_results(args.results_dir)
    
    print("\nGenerating figures...")
    plot_top1_comparison(output, args.output_dir)
    plot_fpr_comparison(output, args.output_dir)
    plot_coordination_strength_sweep(output, args.output_dir)
    plot_score_distributions(output, args.output_dir)
    
    write_summary_csv(output, TABLES_DIR)
    write_results_interpretation(output, PROJECT_ROOT / "docs")
    
    print("\nAnalysis complete.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""
src/llm_helpers.py
==================
Pure helper functions and state abstractions for LLM agent tournaments.
Independent of cloud SDKs (Modal) so that core parsing and state logic
can be tested and run in any environment with zero external dependencies.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
from typing import List, Optional

VALID_ACTIONS: List[str] = ["ROCK", "PAPER", "SCISSORS"]
COUNTER_ACTIONS = {"ROCK": "PAPER", "PAPER": "SCISSORS", "SCISSORS": "ROCK"}


def determine_public_state(previous_actions: Optional[List[str]]) -> str:
    """
    Computes the 5-state low-cardinality public summary for round t
    based strictly on plurality of previous actions.
    Returns: "START", "ROCK", "PAPER", "SCISSORS", or "TIE".
    """
    if not previous_actions:
        return "START"
    counts = {"ROCK": 0, "PAPER": 0, "SCISSORS": 0}
    for a in previous_actions:
        if a in counts:
            counts[a] += 1
    max_c = max(counts.values())
    top = [k for k, v in counts.items() if v == max_c]
    if len(top) == 1:
        return top[0]
    return "TIE"


def parse_action(raw_text: str) -> Optional[str]:
    """
    Robustly parses an action from model response.
    Never defaults to ROCK silently. Returns None if invalid or ambiguous.
    """
    if not raw_text:
        return None
    clean = raw_text.strip()
    if "```" in clean:
        parts = clean.split("```")
        clean = parts[1].replace("json", "").strip() if len(parts) > 1 else parts[0].strip()
    try:
        data = json.loads(clean)
        if isinstance(data, dict):
            act = str(data.get("action", "")).upper().strip()
            if act in VALID_ACTIONS:
                return act
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Secondary regex search for {"action": "..."}
    match = re.search(r'["\']action["\']\s*:\s*["\'](ROCK|PAPER|SCISSORS)["\']', clean, re.IGNORECASE)
    if match:
        return match.group(1).upper()

    # Fallback: exact single word action match (reject if ambiguous)
    words = re.findall(r"\b(ROCK|PAPER|SCISSORS)\b", clean.upper())
    if len(set(words)) == 1:
        return words[0]

    return None

"""
tests/test_analytical_cmi.py
============================
Analytically verified test cases for mutual information and conditional mutual information:
1. Degenerate deterministic policies: Mathematically I(A_i; A_j | S) = 0 when actions are functions of S.
2. Known shared hidden signal: Genuine conditional dependence produces high CMI (~log2(3) bits).
3. Conditional independence with strong unconditional association through S (shared context).
4. Independent random policies: Both marginal and conditional MI near zero.
5. Sensitivity to Laplace smoothing parameter lambda in {0.1, 0.5, 1.0}.
"""

import sys
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))

import numpy as np
import pytest
from src.detectors import _marginal_mi, _conditional_mi


class TestAnalyticalCMI:
    def test_degenerate_deterministic_policy_mathematical_zero(self):
        """When actions are deterministic functions of S, true CMI is 0.
        
        Even under finite-sample Laplace smoothing, as sample count per state increases,
        smoothed single-state MI for deterministic actions approaches 0.
        """
        states = [0, 1, 2] * 200  # 600 samples, 200 per state
        # A0 and A1 are deterministic functions of S
        a0 = [s % 3 for s in states]
        a1 = [s % 3 for s in states]
        
        # Marginal MI is non-zero because actions vary across states
        m_mi = _marginal_mi(a0, a1, pseudocount=0.5)
        assert m_mi > 1.0  # H(S) is large
        
        # When sample count per state is large (200), single-state smoothed MI decays
        cmi_200 = _conditional_mi(a0, a1, states, pseudocount=0.5)
        # Compare to N=10 per state
        states_short = [0, 1, 2] * 10
        cmi_10 = _conditional_mi([s % 3 for s in states_short], [s % 3 for s in states_short], states_short, pseudocount=0.5)
        
        # Proves that the non-zero value at small N is a smoothing artifact that decreases with N
        assert cmi_200 < cmi_10

    def test_known_shared_hidden_signal_positive_cmi(self):
        """When agents share a private signal Z not in S, true CMI is ~log2(3) = 1.585 bits."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
        n = 2000
        states = rng.integers(0, 5, n).tolist()
        # Latent secret signal Z
        Z = rng.integers(0, 3, n).tolist()
        
        a0 = Z[:]
        a1 = Z[:]
        # Independent agent
        a2 = rng.integers(0, 3, n).tolist()
        
        cmi_coord = _conditional_mi(a0, a1, states, pseudocount=0.5)
        cmi_indep = _conditional_mi(a0, a2, states, pseudocount=0.5)
        
        # Coordinating pair has CMI close to log2(3) = 1.585
        assert cmi_coord > 1.3
        # Independent pair has CMI near zero
        assert cmi_indep < 0.05
        # Net influence difference is decisive
        assert cmi_coord - cmi_indep > 1.25

    def test_conditional_independence_with_strong_unconditional_association(self):
        """Shared context generates high Marginal MI but near-zero CMI."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(54321)
        n = 1500
        states = rng.integers(0, 3, n).tolist()
        
        # Both agents best-respond to state S with 10% independent noise
        a0 = [(s + 1) % 3 if rng.random() > 0.1 else int(rng.integers(0, 3)) for s in states]
        a1 = [(s + 1) % 3 if rng.random() > 0.1 else int(rng.integers(0, 3)) for s in states]
        
        marginal_mi = _marginal_mi(a0, a1, pseudocount=0.5)
        cond_mi = _conditional_mi(a0, a1, states, pseudocount=0.5)
        
        # Marginal MI is large (spurious correlation)
        assert marginal_mi > 0.60
        # Conditional MI collapses near zero (conditional independence)
        assert cond_mi < 0.05
        assert marginal_mi > 10 * cond_mi

    def test_independent_random_policies(self):
        """Independent random policies yield near zero for both metrics."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(999)
        n = 1000
        states = rng.integers(0, 5, n).tolist()
        a0 = rng.integers(0, 3, n).tolist()
        a1 = rng.integers(0, 3, n).tolist()
        
        marginal_mi = _marginal_mi(a0, a1, pseudocount=0.5)
        cond_mi = _conditional_mi(a0, a1, states, pseudocount=0.5)
        
        assert marginal_mi < 0.05
        assert cond_mi < 0.05

    def test_smoothing_parameter_sensitivity(self):
        """Verify behavior across smoothing levels lambda in {0.1, 0.5, 1.0}."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(42)
        n = 500
        states = rng.integers(0, 3, n).tolist()
        Z = rng.integers(0, 3, n).tolist()
        
        for pseudocount in [0.1, 0.5, 1.0]:
            cmi = _conditional_mi(Z, Z, states, pseudocount=pseudocount)
            # CMI remains high and positive across all smoothing levels
            assert cmi > 1.0
            assert np.isfinite(cmi)

    def test_joint_coordination_gate_and_frequency(self):
        """Verify joint gate draws G_t ~ Bernoulli(alpha) and coordination frequency matches alpha."""
        from src.trajectories import run_trajectory
        
        # Test Condition C: always active (alpha = 1.0)
        traj_c = run_trajectory(condition="C", seed=42, n_rounds=500)
        rounds_c = traj_c["rounds"]
        # In Condition C, pair (0, 1) always receives private signal and plays identically
        matches_c = sum(1 for r in rounds_c if r["actions"][0] == r["actions"][1])
        assert matches_c == 500
        
        # Test Condition D: joint gate frequency across alpha values
        for alpha in [0.25, 0.50, 0.75]:
            n_rounds = 4000
            traj_d = run_trajectory(condition="D", seed=123, n_rounds=n_rounds, alpha=alpha)
            # Both agents 0 and 1 play identical action whenever the joint gate G_t was active
            # (plus occasional accidental matches during fallback with prob ~1/3 * (1-alpha))
            # Let's verify by re-running the exact generator with the same seed:
            cartel_rng = np.random.default_rng([123, 0xC0110DE])
            gates = [cartel_rng.random() < alpha for _ in range(n_rounds)]
            emp_gate_freq = sum(gates) / n_rounds
            assert abs(emp_gate_freq - alpha) < 0.02

"""
test_detectors.py
=================
Unit tests for the pairwise net influence detector and MI baseline.

Tests verify correctness on synthetic distributions with KNOWN analytical values.
"""

import sys
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))

import numpy as np
import pytest
from src.detectors import (
    _marginal_mi,
    _conditional_mi,
    PairwiseNetInfluenceDetector,
    MarginalMIDetector,
    ALL_PAIRS,
    PSEUDOCOUNT,
    N_AGENTS,
)
from src.environment import ROCK, PAPER, SCISSORS, N_ACTIONS


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tests for _marginal_mi
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestMarginalMI:
    """Tests for unconditional mutual information estimator."""

    def test_independent_uniform_variables_near_zero(self):
        """I(X; Y) ≈ 0 for large samples of independent uniform variables."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
        xs = rng.integers(0, 3, 2000).tolist()
        ys = rng.integers(0, 3, 2000).tolist()
        mi = _marginal_mi(xs, ys)
        # With large N, smoothed estimate should be small (< 0.05 bits)
        assert mi < 0.05, f"Expected near-zero MI, got {mi:.4f}"

    def test_identical_variables_high_mi(self):
        """I(X; X) should be large — equal to H(X) ≈ log2(3) ≈ 1.585 bits."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(1)
        xs = rng.integers(0, 3, 1000).tolist()
        mi = _marginal_mi(xs, xs)  # perfect dependence
        # H(uniform over 3) = log2(3) ≈ 1.585; expect MI close to this
        assert mi > 1.4, f"Expected high MI for X=Y, got {mi:.4f}"

    def test_perfectly_correlated_pair_vs_independent(self):
        """Perfectly correlated pair should have higher MI than independent."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(2)
        xs = rng.integers(0, 3, 500).tolist()
        ys_dep = xs[:]            # perfect dependence
        ys_ind = rng.integers(0, 3, 500).tolist()  # independent
        mi_dep = _marginal_mi(xs, ys_dep)
        mi_ind = _marginal_mi(xs, ys_ind)
        assert mi_dep > mi_ind + 0.5, \
            f"Dep MI={mi_dep:.4f}, Ind MI={mi_ind:.4f}; dep should be higher"

    def test_non_negative(self):
        """MI must always be non-negative."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(3)
        for _ in range(20):
            xs = rng.integers(0, 3, rng.integers(5, 50)).tolist()
            ys = rng.integers(0, 3, len(xs)).tolist()
            assert _marginal_mi(xs, ys) >= 0.0


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tests for _conditional_mi
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestConditionalMI:
    """Tests for conditional mutual information estimator."""

    def test_independent_given_state_near_zero(self):
        """I(X; Y | S) ≈ 0 when X ⊥ Y | S for large samples."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(10)
        n = 1000
        # State determines the marginal distribution; both agents sample independently
        states = rng.integers(0, 3, n).tolist()
        xs = [rng.integers(0, 3) for _ in states]  # independent of y given state
        ys = [rng.integers(0, 3) for _ in states]
        cmi = _conditional_mi(xs, ys, states)
        assert cmi < 0.05, f"Expected near-zero CMI for independent agents, got {cmi:.4f}"

    def test_coordinated_given_state_high_cmi(self):
        """I(X; Y | S) is large when both agents play the SAME action (coordination)."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(11)
        n = 500
        states = rng.integers(0, 3, n).tolist()
        # Both agents always play state % 3 (perfect coordination)
        coord_action = [s % 3 for s in states]
        cmi = _conditional_mi(coord_action, coord_action, states)
        # Should be large; with uniform state, ≈ H(action|state) = H(coord|state) ≈ 0
        # but since action is fully determined by state, CMI should detect residual correlation
        # In this edge case, all variance is explained by state; actual CMI of identical 
        # deterministic variables is H(X|S=s) for each s — which is 0 when deterministic.
        # So let's test a STOCHASTIC coordination case instead:
        pass

    def test_stochastic_coordination_higher_than_independent(self):
        """CMI of coordinated pair > CMI of independent pair, for same state exposure."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12)
        n = 800
        states = [tuple(rng.integers(0, 3, 4).tolist()) for _ in range(n)]
        
        # Coordinated: both play same action (derived from a shared source)
        shared = rng.integers(0, 3, n)
        coord_x = shared.tolist()
        coord_y = shared.tolist()
        
        # Independent: sample separately
        indep_x = rng.integers(0, 3, n).tolist()
        indep_y = rng.integers(0, 3, n).tolist()
        
        cmi_coord = _conditional_mi(coord_x, coord_y, states)
        cmi_indep = _conditional_mi(indep_x, indep_y, states)
        
        assert cmi_coord > cmi_indep, \
            f"Coordinated CMI={cmi_coord:.4f} should exceed independent CMI={cmi_indep:.4f}"

    def test_cmi_non_negative(self):
        """CMI must always be non-negative."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(13)
        for _ in range(20):
            n = rng.integers(10, 100)
            states = rng.integers(0, 3, n).tolist()
            xs = rng.integers(0, 3, n).tolist()
            ys = rng.integers(0, 3, n).tolist()
            assert _conditional_mi(xs, ys, states) >= 0.0

    def test_empty_state_list_returns_zero(self):
        """Empty input should return 0 gracefully."""
        result = _conditional_mi([], [], [])
        assert result == 0.0


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tests for PairwiseNetInfluenceDetector
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestPairwiseNetInfluenceDetector:
    """Integration tests for the full detector pipeline."""

    def _make_rounds(self, actions_seq: list[list[int]]) -> list[dict]:
        """Build detector-visible round records from a list of joint action tuples."""
        rounds = []
        for t, actions in enumerate(actions_seq):
            state = ("START",) if t == 0 else tuple(actions_seq[t-1])
            rounds.append({
                "round_idx": t,
                "state_feature": state,
                "actions": actions,
                "payoffs": [0.0] * N_AGENTS,
            })
        return rounds

    def test_returns_six_pairs(self):
        """Detector must score all 6 unordered pairs."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(20)
        actions_seq = [rng.integers(0, 3, 4).tolist() for _ in range(100)]
        rounds = self._make_rounds(actions_seq)
        det = PairwiseNetInfluenceDetector()
        results = det.score_pairs(rounds)
        assert len(results) == 6, f"Expected 6 pairs, got {len(results)}"

    def test_scores_are_finite(self):
        """All scores should be finite floats."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(21)
        actions_seq = [rng.integers(0, 3, 4).tolist() for _ in range(100)]
        rounds = self._make_rounds(actions_seq)
        det = PairwiseNetInfluenceDetector()
        results = det.score_pairs(rounds)
        for r in results:
            assert np.isfinite(r["score"]), f"Non-finite score for pair {r['pair']}: {r['score']}"

    def test_sorted_descending(self):
        """Results must be sorted descending by score."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(22)
        actions_seq = [rng.integers(0, 3, 4).tolist() for _ in range(100)]
        rounds = self._make_rounds(actions_seq)
        det = PairwiseNetInfluenceDetector()
        results = det.score_pairs(rounds)
        scores = [r["score"] for r in results]
        assert scores == sorted(scores, reverse=True)

    def test_coordinated_pair_ranks_first_or_second(self):
        """For strong coordination, the true pair should rank high."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(23)
        n = 500
        # Build strongly coordinated trajectory: agents 0 and 1 always play same action
        # derived from a shared random source; agents 2 and 3 play independently
        actions_seq = []
        prev = None
        for t in range(n):
            state = prev if prev is not None else [0, 0, 0, 0]
            # Shared signal for pair (0,1)
            coord_action = int(rng.integers(0, 3))
            a0 = coord_action
            a1 = coord_action
            a2 = int(rng.integers(0, 3))
            a3 = int(rng.integers(0, 3))
            actions_seq.append([a0, a1, a2, a3])
            prev = [a0, a1, a2, a3]
        
        rounds = self._make_rounds(actions_seq)
        det = PairwiseNetInfluenceDetector()
        results = det.score_pairs(rounds)
        
        # True pair (0,1) should be in top 2
        ranked_pairs = [frozenset(r["pair"]) for r in results[:2]]
        assert frozenset({0, 1}) in ranked_pairs, \
            f"True pair not in top 2. Ranking: {[r['pair'] for r in results]}"

    def test_insufficient_data_returns_nan(self):
        """Single round should return nan scores."""
        rounds = [{"round_idx": 0, "state_feature": ("START",), "actions": [0, 1, 2, 0], "payoffs": [0, 0, 0, 0]}]
        det = PairwiseNetInfluenceDetector()
        results = det.score_pairs(rounds)
        # Should return error entries or nan
        for r in results:
            assert "error" in r or np.isnan(r["score"])

    def test_gamma_matrix_symmetric(self):
        """gamma(i,j) should equal gamma(j,i) by symmetry of CMI."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(24)
        actions_seq = [rng.integers(0, 3, 4).tolist() for _ in range(200)]
        rounds = self._make_rounds(actions_seq)
        det = PairwiseNetInfluenceDetector()
        gamma = det.compute_gamma_matrix(rounds)
        # Check symmetry within tolerance
        for i in range(N_AGENTS):
            for j in range(N_AGENTS):
                if i != j:
                    assert abs(gamma[i, j] - gamma[j, i]) < 0.01, \
                        f"gamma[{i},{j}]={gamma[i,j]:.6f} != gamma[{j},{i}]={gamma[j,i]:.6f}"

class TestMarginalMIDetector:
    """Tests for the naive baseline detector."""

    def _make_rounds(self, actions_seq):
        return [
            {"round_idx": t, "state_feature": ("START",) if t == 0 else tuple(a),
             "actions": a, "payoffs": [0.0] * N_AGENTS}
            for t, a in enumerate(actions_seq)
        ]

    def test_shared_context_inflates_score(self):
        """Naive MI should score shared-context agents highly (false positive scenario)."""
        from src.agents import make_condition_b_agents
        from src.environment import RPSGame
        
        rng = np.random.default_rng(30)
        game = RPSGame(n_rounds=300, rng=rng)
        agents = make_condition_b_agents(base_seed=30, noise=0.05)
        history = game.run(agents)
        
        det = MarginalMIDetector()
        results = det.score_pairs(history)
        
        # All pairs should have moderate scores due to shared state conditioning
        scores = [r["score"] for r in results]
        assert max(scores) > 0.05, "Expected inflated MI scores under shared context"

    def test_returns_six_pairs(self):
        """Baseline detector must score all 6 pairs."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(31)
        actions_seq = [rng.integers(0, 3, 4).tolist() for _ in range(50)]
        rounds = self._make_rounds(actions_seq)
        det = MarginalMIDetector()
        results = det.score_pairs(rounds)
        assert len(results) == 6


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Test: Ground truth labels never consumed by detectors
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestGroundTruthSeparation:
    """Verify that detector code never sees ground-truth labels."""

    def test_detector_has_no_gt_field_in_input(self):
        """Detector input (rounds) must not contain 'ground_truth' or 'coordinating_pair'."""
        from src.trajectories import run_trajectory, save_trajectory, load_trajectory
        from pathlib import Path
        import tempfile
        
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:
            data_dir = Path(tmpdir)
            traj = run_trajectory("C", seed=0, n_rounds=50)
            traj_path, gt_path = save_trajectory(traj, data_dir, overwrite=True)
            meta, rounds = load_trajectory(traj_path)
            
            # Check that rounds don't contain GT labels
            for r in rounds:
                assert "coordinating_pair" not in r
                assert "ground_truth" not in r
                assert "gt_pair" not in r
            
            # Check that meta doesn't contain GT labels  
            assert "coordinating_pair" not in meta or meta.get("coordinating_pair") is None

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestConditionSanityChecks:
    """Sanity checks that conditions produce expected behavior patterns."""

    def _run_condition(self, condition, seed=42, n_rounds=300, alpha=1.0):
        from src.trajectories import run_trajectory
        traj = run_trajectory(condition, seed=seed, n_rounds=n_rounds, alpha=alpha)
        return traj["rounds"]

    def test_condition_a_agents_play_valid_actions(self):
        """All actions in Condition A must be in {0, 1, 2}."""
        rounds = self._run_condition("A")
        for r in rounds:
            for a in r["actions"]:
                assert a in (0, 1, 2), f"Invalid action {a}"

    def test_condition_c_coordinated_pair_often_plays_same_action(self):
        """Under full coordination (alpha=1), agents 0 and 1 always play the same action."""
        rounds = self._run_condition("C", alpha=1.0)
        agreement = sum(1 for r in rounds if r["actions"][0] == r["actions"][1])
        total = len(rounds)
        agreement_rate = agreement / total
        # With alpha=1.0 (full coordination), they should ALWAYS agree
        assert agreement_rate == 1.0, \
            f"Fully coordinated pair should always agree, got {agreement_rate:.2%}"

    def test_condition_d_partial_coordination(self):
        """Under partial coordination (alpha=0.5), agreement rate should be between chance and 1."""
        rounds = self._run_condition("D", alpha=0.5)
        agreement = sum(1 for r in rounds if r["actions"][0] == r["actions"][1])
        total = len(rounds)
        agreement_rate = agreement / total
        # Chance agreement = 1/3; should be above chance but below 1
        assert agreement_rate > 1/3 + 0.05, \
            f"Partial coordination should exceed chance ({1/3:.2f}), got {agreement_rate:.2%}"

    def test_condition_a_no_shared_state(self):
        """Condition A agents should not share mutable state across rounds."""
        from src.agents import make_condition_a_agents
        # Create agents with two different seeds; verify different trajectories
        agents1 = make_condition_a_agents(base_seed=100)
        agents2 = make_condition_a_agents(base_seed=200)
        
        from src.environment import RPSGameState
        state = RPSGameState(round_idx=0)
        
        # Sample 10 actions from each set
        actions1 = [a(state, i) for i, a in enumerate(agents1)]
        actions2 = [a(state, i) for i, a in enumerate(agents2)]
        
        # Different seeds should produce different action profiles (with high probability)
        # This is not guaranteed with probability 1, but with seeds 100 vs 200 it holds
        assert actions1 != actions2, "Different seeds should produce different actions"

    def test_seed_reproducibility(self):
        """Same seed should produce identical trajectory."""
        rounds_a = self._run_condition("C", seed=42)
        rounds_b = self._run_condition("C", seed=42)
        
        for ra, rb in zip(rounds_a, rounds_b):
            assert ra["actions"] == rb["actions"], \
                f"Seed 42 should be reproducible. Got {ra['actions']} vs {rb['actions']}"


class TestConditionalMIDetector:
    """Tests for the raw Conditional MI baseline detector."""

    def test_cmi_detector_keys_and_ranking(self):
        from src.detectors import ConditionalMIDetector, run_detectors
        from src.trajectories import run_trajectory
        traj = run_trajectory("C", seed=42, n_rounds=50)
        rounds = traj["rounds"]
        
        cmi_det = ConditionalMIDetector(threshold=0.01)
        results = cmi_det.score_pairs(rounds)
        
        assert len(results) == 6
        for r in results:
            assert "pair" in r
            assert "score" in r
            assert "flagged" in r
            assert not np.isnan(r["score"])

        # Check run_detectors returns all three detectors
        all_res = run_detectors(rounds, threshold={"pni": 0.01, "cmi": 0.02, "marginal_mi": 0.03})
        assert "pni" in all_res
        assert "cmi" in all_res
        assert "marginal_mi" in all_res
        assert len(all_res["cmi"]) == 6


class TestEstimatorSmoothingSensitivity:
    """Tests evaluating estimator behavior across regularization choices (pseudocounts)."""

    def test_unsmoothed_plugin_zero_log_zero_convention(self):
        """Unsmoothed plug-in estimator (pseudocount=0.0) should execute without NaN or error."""
        from src.detectors import _marginal_mi, _conditional_mi
        # Empty inputs
        assert _marginal_mi([], [], pseudocount=0.0) == 0.0
        assert _conditional_mi([], [], [], pseudocount=0.0) == 0.0

        # Deterministic relationship
        xs = [0, 0, 1, 1, 2, 2]
        ys = [0, 0, 1, 1, 2, 2]
        states = ["s1", "s1", "s2", "s2", "s3", "s3"]
        mi_0 = _marginal_mi(xs, ys, pseudocount=0.0)
        cmi_0 = _conditional_mi(xs, ys, states, pseudocount=0.0)
        assert not np.isnan(mi_0)
        assert not np.isnan(cmi_0)
        assert mi_0 > 1.0

    def test_pseudocount_sensitivity_ordering(self):
        """Comparing pseudocounts 0.0, 0.5, and 1.0 shows consistent finite-sample regularization."""
        from src.detectors import _conditional_mi
        from src.trajectories import run_trajectory
        traj = run_trajectory("B", seed=0, n_rounds=100)
        rounds = traj["rounds"]
        states = [r["state_feature"] for r in rounds]
        actions = np.array([r["actions"] for r in rounds])
        
        cmi_0 = _conditional_mi(actions[:, 0].tolist(), actions[:, 1].tolist(), states, pseudocount=0.0)
        cmi_05 = _conditional_mi(actions[:, 0].tolist(), actions[:, 1].tolist(), states, pseudocount=0.5)
        cmi_10 = _conditional_mi(actions[:, 0].tolist(), actions[:, 1].tolist(), states, pseudocount=1.0)

        assert not np.isnan(cmi_0)
        assert not np.isnan(cmi_05)
        assert not np.isnan(cmi_10)
        # Regularization introduces small variation in sparse cells
        assert abs(cmi_05 - cmi_0) < 0.1
        assert abs(cmi_10 - cmi_05) < 0.1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    pytest.main([__file__, "-v"])

"""
test_environment.py
==================
Unit tests for the RPS game environment.
"""
import sys
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))

import numpy as np
import pytest
from src.environment import (
    RPSGame, RPSGameState, compute_payoffs,
    ROCK, PAPER, SCISSORS, N_AGENTS, PAYOFF_MATRIX
)


class TestPayoffs:
    """Test payoff calculation on hand-computed examples."""

    def test_rock_beats_scissors(self):
        """Rock vs Scissors: winner gets +3, loser gets -3 (vs 3 opponents)."""
        # All 4 agents: agent 0 plays Rock, all others play Scissors
        actions = [ROCK, SCISSORS, SCISSORS, SCISSORS]
        payoffs = compute_payoffs(actions)
        # Agent 0 (Rock) beats 3 Scissors: +3
        assert payoffs[0] == 3.0
        # Each Scissors loses to Rock: -1 (1 match), but beats no one here
        # Actually: Scissors loses to Rock (+1 for Rock = -1 for Scissors)
        for p in payoffs[1:]:
            assert p == -1.0

    def test_all_same_action_zero_payoffs(self):
        """All agents playing same action → all payoffs = 0."""
        for action in [ROCK, PAPER, SCISSORS]:
            actions = [action] * N_AGENTS
            payoffs = compute_payoffs(actions)
            assert all(p == 0.0 for p in payoffs), f"Expected zero payoffs, got {payoffs}"

    def test_paper_beats_rock(self):
        """Paper beats Rock."""
        # Agent 0 plays Paper, rest play Rock
        actions = [PAPER, ROCK, ROCK, ROCK]
        payoffs = compute_payoffs(actions)
        assert payoffs[0] == 3.0  # beats 3 rocks
        for p in payoffs[1:]:
            assert p == -1.0  # each rock loses to paper

    def test_scissors_beats_paper(self):
        """Scissors beats Paper."""
        actions = [SCISSORS, PAPER, PAPER, PAPER]
        payoffs = compute_payoffs(actions)
        assert payoffs[0] == 3.0
        for p in payoffs[1:]:
            assert p == -1.0

    def test_payoffs_sum_to_zero(self):
        """Total payoffs across agents must sum to zero (zero-sum game)."""
        rng = np.random.default_rng(42)
        for _ in range(100):
            actions = rng.integers(0, 3, 4).tolist()
            payoffs = compute_payoffs(actions)
            assert abs(sum(payoffs)) < 1e-9, f"Non-zero sum: {payoffs}"

    def test_invalid_action_raises(self):
        """Invalid action should raise AssertionError."""
        with pytest.raises(AssertionError):
            compute_payoffs([0, 1, 2, 3])  # 3 is invalid


class TestRPSGame:
    """Tests for the game orchestrator."""

    def test_correct_round_count(self):
        """Game runs exactly n_rounds rounds."""
        game = RPSGame(n_rounds=10)
        from src.agents import make_condition_a_agents
        agents = make_condition_a_agents(base_seed=0)
        history = game.run(agents)
        assert len(history) == 10

    def test_state_transitions(self):
        """State at round t+1 should be previous round's actions."""
        game = RPSGame(n_rounds=5)
        from src.agents import make_condition_a_agents
        agents = make_condition_a_agents(base_seed=1)
        history = game.run(agents)
        
        # Round 0 should have START state
        assert history[0]["state_feature"] == ("START",)
        
        # Round 1's state should be round 0's actions
        assert tuple(history[0]["actions"]) == history[1]["state_feature"]

    def test_reset_clears_history(self):
        """reset() should clear the game history."""
        game = RPSGame(n_rounds=5)
        from src.agents import make_condition_a_agents
        agents = make_condition_a_agents(base_seed=2)
        game.run(agents)
        game.reset()
        assert len(game.history) == 0

    def test_seed_reproducibility(self):
        """Same seed produces identical history."""
        from src.agents import make_condition_a_agents
        
        game1 = RPSGame(n_rounds=20)
        agents1 = make_condition_a_agents(base_seed=99)
        history1 = game1.run(agents1)
        
        game2 = RPSGame(n_rounds=20)
        agents2 = make_condition_a_agents(base_seed=99)
        history2 = game2.run(agents2)
        
        assert [r["actions"] for r in history1] == [r["actions"] for r in history2]

    def test_actions_are_valid(self):
        """All actions in game history must be in {0, 1, 2}."""
        game = RPSGame(n_rounds=50)
        from src.agents import make_condition_a_agents
        agents = make_condition_a_agents(base_seed=3)
        history = game.run(agents)
        
        for record in history:
            for action in record["actions"]:
                assert action in (ROCK, PAPER, SCISSORS), f"Invalid action {action}"

    def test_is_done_after_run(self):
        """Game should be done after running all rounds."""
        game = RPSGame(n_rounds=5)
        from src.agents import make_condition_a_agents
        agents = make_condition_a_agents(base_seed=4)
        game.run(agents)
        assert game.is_done

    def test_step_raises_when_done(self):
        """Step on a finished game should raise RuntimeError."""
        game = RPSGame(n_rounds=1)
        game.step([ROCK, ROCK, ROCK, ROCK])
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
            game.step([ROCK, ROCK, ROCK, ROCK])


class TestRPSGameState:
    """Tests for the state object."""

    def test_start_state_hashable(self):
        """State feature must be hashable for use as dict key."""
        state = RPSGameState(round_idx=0)
        d = {state.to_feature(): "value"}  # should not raise

    def test_non_start_state_hashable(self):
        """Non-start state with action tuple must be hashable."""
        state = RPSGameState(round_idx=1, prev_actions=(0, 1, 2, 0))
        d = {state.to_feature(): "value"}  # should not raise

    pytest.main([__file__, "-v"])

"""
tests/test_metrics.py
=====================
Unit tests for ranking, accuracy, false positive rate, and bootstrap CI metrics.
"""

import sys
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))

import numpy as np
import pytest
from src.metrics import (
    top_k_accuracy,
    reciprocal_rank,
    true_pair_score,
    false_positive_rate,
    chance_top1_accuracy,
    aggregate_metrics,
)


class TestMetrics:
    def test_top1_accuracy_success(self):
        ranked = [{"pair": (0, 1), "score": 0.5}, {"pair": (0, 2), "score": 0.1}]
        assert top_k_accuracy(ranked, (0, 1), k=1) == 1.0
        assert top_k_accuracy(ranked, (1, 0), k=1) == 1.0  # order agnostic

    def test_top1_accuracy_failure(self):
        ranked = [{"pair": (0, 2), "score": 0.5}, {"pair": (0, 1), "score": 0.1}]
        assert top_k_accuracy(ranked, (0, 1), k=1) == 0.0
        assert top_k_accuracy(ranked, (0, 1), k=2) == 1.0

    def test_reciprocal_rank(self):
        ranked = [
            {"pair": (0, 2), "score": 0.5},
            {"pair": (1, 2), "score": 0.4},
            {"pair": (0, 1), "score": 0.3},
        ]
        assert reciprocal_rank(ranked, (0, 1)) == pytest.approx(1.0 / 3.0)
        assert reciprocal_rank(ranked, (0, 2)) == pytest.approx(1.0)
        assert reciprocal_rank(ranked, (2, 3)) == 0.0

    def test_false_positive_rate(self):
        # 6 candidate pairs total, pair (0, 1) is true
        ranked = [
            {"pair": (0, 1), "score": 0.5, "flagged": True},
            {"pair": (0, 2), "score": 0.2, "flagged": True},
            {"pair": (0, 3), "score": -0.1, "flagged": False},
            {"pair": (1, 2), "score": -0.2, "flagged": False},
            {"pair": (1, 3), "score": -0.3, "flagged": False},
            {"pair": (2, 3), "score": -0.4, "flagged": False},
        ]
        # Negatives are the 5 pairs other than (0, 1). Only (0, 2) is flagged.
        fpr = false_positive_rate(ranked, threshold=0.0, true_pair=(0, 1))
        assert fpr == pytest.approx(1.0 / 5.0)

    def test_chance_top1(self):
        assert chance_top1_accuracy(6) == pytest.approx(1.0 / 6.0)

    def test_bootstrap_aggregation(self):
        results = [{"score": 1.0}, {"score": 2.0}, {"score": 3.0}, {"score": 4.0}, {"score": 5.0}]
        agg = aggregate_metrics(results, bootstrap_n=500, confidence=0.95)
        assert "score" in agg
        assert agg["score"]["mean"] == pytest.approx(3.0)
        assert agg["score"]["ci_low"] <= agg["score"]["mean"]
        assert agg["score"]["ci_high"] >= agg["score"]["mean"]
        assert agg["score"]["n"] == 5

"""
Unit tests for the exploratory LLM pilot helper functions and state abstraction.
Imports pure logic from src.llm_helpers with zero cloud dependencies.
"""

import pytest
from src.llm_helpers import determine_public_state, parse_action, VALID_ACTIONS, COUNTER_ACTIONS


def test_determine_public_state_start():
    assert determine_public_state(None) == "START"
    assert determine_public_state([]) == "START"


def test_determine_public_state_plurality():
    # Strict plurality
    assert determine_public_state(["ROCK", "ROCK", "PAPER", "SCISSORS"]) == "ROCK"
    assert determine_public_state(["PAPER", "PAPER", "PAPER", "ROCK"]) == "PAPER"
    assert determine_public_state(["SCISSORS", "SCISSORS", "ROCK", "PAPER"]) == "SCISSORS"


def test_determine_public_state_tie():
    # 2 vs 2 tie
    assert determine_public_state(["ROCK", "ROCK", "PAPER", "PAPER"]) == "TIE"
    assert determine_public_state(["ROCK", "ROCK", "SCISSORS", "SCISSORS"]) == "TIE"
    assert determine_public_state(["PAPER", "PAPER", "SCISSORS", "SCISSORS"]) == "TIE"


def test_counter_actions_validity():
    for act in VALID_ACTIONS:
        assert act in COUNTER_ACTIONS
        counter = COUNTER_ACTIONS[act]
        assert counter in VALID_ACTIONS
        assert counter != act


def test_parse_action_valid():
    assert parse_action('{"action": "ROCK"}') == "ROCK"
    assert parse_action('{"action": "PAPER"}') == "PAPER"
    assert parse_action('{"action": "SCISSORS"}') == "SCISSORS"
    assert parse_action('```json\n{"action": "ROCK"}\n```') == "ROCK"
    assert parse_action('{"action": "rock"}') == "ROCK"


def test_parse_action_fallback_and_invalid():
    assert parse_action("I choose ROCK for this round.") == "ROCK"
    # Never silently default to ROCK if invalid
    assert parse_action("I surrender and pass.") is None
    assert parse_action("") is None
    assert parse_action("invalid output") is None
    # Ambiguous choices should return None
    assert parse_action("Either ROCK or PAPER") is None
