import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path
import docx
from docx import Document
from docx.shared import Inches, Pt, RGBColor
from docx.enum.text import WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH
from docx.enum.table import WD_TABLE_ALIGNMENT
from docx.oxml import parse_xml
from docx.oxml.ns import nsdecls

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
LOCAL_TEMPLATE = PROJECT_ROOT / "docs" / "template.docx"
SCRATCH_TEMPLATE = PROJECT_ROOT.parent / "template.docx"
TEMPLATE_PATH = LOCAL_TEMPLATE if LOCAL_TEMPLATE.exists() else SCRATCH_TEMPLATE
REPORT_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "report"
OUTPUT_DOCX = REPORT_DIR / "when_correlation_lies_report.docx"
OUTPUT_PDF = REPORT_DIR / "when_correlation_lies_report.pdf"
FIGURES_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "results" / "figures"


def set_cell_background(cell, fill_hex):
    """Sets background color of a table cell."""
    tcPr = cell._tc.get_or_add_tcPr()
    shd = parse_xml(f'<w:shd {nsdecls("w")} w:fill="{fill_hex}"/>')
    tcPr.append(shd)


def set_cell_margins(cell, top=35, bottom=35, left=60, right=60):
    """Sets cell padding (in twips: 20 twips = 1 pt)."""
    tcPr = cell._tc.get_or_add_tcPr()
    tcMar = parse_xml(
        f'<w:tcMar {nsdecls("w")}>'
        f'  <w:top w:w="{top}" w:type="dxa"/>'
        f'  <w:bottom w:w="{bottom}" w:type="dxa"/>'
        f'  <w:left w:w="{left}" w:type="dxa"/>'
        f'  <w:right w:w="{right}" w:type="dxa"/>'
        f'</w:tcMar>'
    )
    tcPr.append(tcMar)


def set_table_borders(table, color="B0BEC5", sz="4", val="single"):
    """Sets standard clean borders on a table."""
    tblPr = table._tbl.tblPr
    borders = parse_xml(
        f'<w:tblBorders {nsdecls("w")}>'
        f'  <w:top w:val="{val}" w:sz="{sz}" w:space="0" w:color="{color}"/>'
        f'  <w:bottom w:val="{val}" w:sz="{sz}" w:space="0" w:color="{color}"/>'
        f'  <w:left w:val="none"/>'
        f'  <w:right w:val="none"/>'
        f'  <w:insideH w:val="{val}" w:sz="{sz}" w:space="0" w:color="{color}"/>'
        f'  <w:insideV w:val="none"/>'
        f'</w:tblBorders>'
    )
    tblPr.append(borders)


def format_table(table, col_widths, headers, data, align_right_cols=None, font_size=6.6):
    """Formats a full data table with clean header and zebra rows."""
    table.alignment = WD_TABLE_ALIGNMENT.CENTER
    if align_right_cols is None:
        align_right_cols = []

    # Prevent rows from splitting across pages
    for row in table.rows:
        trPr = row._tr.get_or_add_trPr()
        trPr.append(parse_xml(f'<w:cantSplit {nsdecls("w")}/>'))

    # Repeat header row on every page
    header_tr = table.rows[0]._tr.get_or_add_trPr()
    header_tr.append(parse_xml(f'<w:tblHeader {nsdecls("w")}/>'))

    # Header Row
    hdr_cells = table.rows[0].cells
    for i, title in enumerate(headers):
        hdr_cells[i].text = title
        set_cell_background(hdr_cells[i], "1F3A52")
        set_cell_margins(hdr_cells[i], top=50, bottom=50, left=50, right=50)
        p = hdr_cells[i].paragraphs[0]
        p.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(0)
        p.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(0)
        p.paragraph_format.line_spacing = 1.0
        p.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.RIGHT if i in align_right_cols else WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.LEFT
        for run in p.runs:
            run.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
            run.font.size = Pt(font_size)
            run.font.bold = True
            run.font.color.rgb = RGBColor(0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF)

    # Data Rows
    for r_idx, row_data in enumerate(data):
        row = table.rows[r_idx + 1]
        bg_color = "F7F9FA" if r_idx % 2 == 1 else "FFFFFF"
        for c_idx, val in enumerate(row_data):
            cell = row.cells[c_idx]
            cell.text = str(val)
            set_cell_background(cell, bg_color)
            set_cell_margins(cell, top=30, bottom=30, left=50, right=50)
            p = cell.paragraphs[0]
            p.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(0)
            p.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(0)
            p.paragraph_format.line_spacing = 1.0
            p.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.RIGHT if c_idx in align_right_cols else WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.LEFT
            for run in p.runs:
                run.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
                run.font.size = Pt(font_size)
                run.font.color.rgb = RGBColor(0x22, 0x22, 0x22)
                if "**" in val:
                    run.font.bold = True

    # Set column widths
    for row in table.rows:
        for i, w in enumerate(col_widths):
            row.cells[i].width = w

    set_table_borders(table)


def build_official_report():
    print(f"Loading official template from: {TEMPLATE_PATH}")
    doc = Document(str(TEMPLATE_PATH))

    # Page Margins: 0.70 in top/bottom/left/right
    sec = doc.sections[0]
    sec.top_margin = Inches(0.70)
    sec.bottom_margin = Inches(0.70)
    sec.left_margin = Inches(0.70)
    sec.right_margin = Inches(0.70)

    # Remove all placeholder tables (Table 0 and Table 1)
    for t in list(doc.tables):
        t._element.getparent().remove(t._element)

    # Clear all placeholder paragraphs
    for p in list(doc.paragraphs):
        p._element.getparent().remove(p._element)

    # --- 1. TITLE & SUBTITLE ---
    p_title = doc.add_paragraph()
    p_title.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
    p_title.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(0)
    p_title.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(2)
    r_title = p_title.add_run("When Correlation Lies: Stress-Testing Pairwise Net Influence for Detecting Coordination Among Agents")
    r_title.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
    r_title.font.size = Pt(15.5)
    r_title.font.bold = True
    r_title.font.color.rgb = RGBColor(0x1F, 0x3A, 0x52)

    p_sub = doc.add_paragraph()
    p_sub.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
    p_sub.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(0)
    p_sub.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(4)
    r_sub = p_sub.add_run("Track 1.4: Markets and Collusion — Apart Research AI Collusion Research Sprint (October 23–25, 2026)")
    r_sub.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
    r_sub.font.size = Pt(9.0)
    r_sub.font.italic = True
    r_sub.font.color.rgb = RGBColor(0x55, 0x55, 0x55)

    # --- 2. AUTHOR BLOCK ---
    p_auth = doc.add_paragraph()
    p_auth.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
    p_auth.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(0)
    p_auth.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(4)
    
    r_auth = p_auth.add_run("Durgesh Unde\n")
    r_auth.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
    r_auth.font.size = Pt(10.5)
    r_auth.font.bold = True

    r_affil = p_auth.add_run("Independent Researcher\n")
    r_affil.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
    r_affil.font.size = Pt(9.0)
    r_affil.font.italic = True

    r_meta = p_auth.add_run("Verified Pipeline: Python 3.13 / CPU Benchmarks & Modal CPU Cloud ($40.63 Metered, $0.00 Out-of-Pocket)")
    r_meta.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
    r_meta.font.size = Pt(7.8)
    r_meta.font.color.rgb = RGBColor(0x66, 0x66, 0x66)

    # --- 3. ABSTRACT BOX ---
    t_abs = doc.add_table(rows=1, cols=1)
    t_abs.alignment = WD_TABLE_ALIGNMENT.CENTER
    c_abs = t_abs.rows[0].cells[0]
    c_abs.width = Inches(6.8)
    set_cell_background(c_abs, "F0F4F8")
    set_cell_margins(c_abs, top=80, bottom=80, left=120, right=120)

    p_abs_title = c_abs.paragraphs[0]
    p_abs_title.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
    p_abs_title.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(0)
    p_abs_title.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(2)
    r_at = p_abs_title.add_run("ABSTRACT")
    r_at.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
    r_at.font.size = Pt(9.5)
    r_at.font.bold = True
    r_at.font.color.rgb = RGBColor(0x1F, 0x3A, 0x52)

    abstract_text = (
        "As autonomous AI agents are increasingly deployed in high-frequency trading, automated procurement, and algorithmic pricing, "
        "covert collusion poses severe economic and systemic risks. Information-theoretic detectors, notably Pairwise Net Influence (PNI; Bonjour et al., 2022), "
        "measure directed action dependence via Conditional Mutual Information (CMI) conditioned on observable public game state. "
        "However, whether PNI can distinguish deliberate multi-agent coordination from spurious correlation induced by shared context remains an open empirical question. "
        "In this work, we stress-test PNI against unconditioned marginal mutual information and raw conditional mutual information baselines in an iterated 4-agent game across four controlled conditions: "
        "independent play, shared-context confounding, shared-private-signal coordination, and coordination-strength sweeps. "
        "Across 20 independent seeds and trajectory lengths (T in [50, 500]), unconditioned mutual information exhibits a 38-fold surge under shared context, "
        "falsely accusing independent agents of collusion (FPR = 1.000). Conditioning on observable state neutralizes this contextual surge below independent calibration thresholds (CMI FPR = 0.000), "
        "while PNI competitor subtraction isolates coordinating pairs with 100% Top-1 ranking accuracy (Wilson 95% CI: [0.839, 1.000], MRR = 1.000). "
        "Furthermore, expanding evaluations to 68,400 open-weight LLM decisions (Qwen2.5-1.5B, 630 trajectories) across horizons T in {5, 20, 50} reveals an empirical finite-sample sparsity effect: "
        "ultra-short horizons (T=5) attenuate CMI ranking accuracy (70.0%), whereas sufficient history (T >= 20) restores 100% Top-1 identification accuracy under controlled ranking evidence. "
        "Our findings support state conditioning as a useful safeguard in the tested setting while highlighting critical sample horizon boundaries."
    )
    p_abs_body = c_abs.add_paragraph()
    p_abs_body.paragraph_format.line_spacing = 1.08
    p_abs_body.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(0)
    p_abs_body.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(0)
    r_ab = p_abs_body.add_run(abstract_text)
    r_ab.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
    r_ab.font.size = Pt(8.2)
    r_ab.font.italic = True
    r_ab.font.color.rgb = RGBColor(0x22, 0x22, 0x22)

    # Typography Helpers
    def add_h1(text):
        p = doc.add_paragraph(style="Heading 2")
        p.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(5.5)
        p.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(1.5)
        p.paragraph_format.keep_with_next = True
        r = p.add_run(text)
        r.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
        r.font.size = Pt(10.8)
        r.font.bold = True
        r.font.color.rgb = RGBColor(0x1F, 0x3A, 0x52)
        return p

    def add_h2(text):
        p = doc.add_paragraph(style="Heading 3")
        p.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(3.5)
        p.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(1.0)
        p.paragraph_format.keep_with_next = True
        r = p.add_run(text)
        r.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
        r.font.size = Pt(9.5)
        r.font.bold = True
        r.font.color.rgb = RGBColor(0x2E, 0x5A, 0x7E)
        return p

    def add_p(text, bold_prefix=None, space_after=1.5, font_size=8.8, line_spacing=1.05):
        p = doc.add_paragraph()
        p.paragraph_format.line_spacing = line_spacing
        p.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(0)
        p.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(space_after)
        if bold_prefix:
            r_b = p.add_run(bold_prefix)
            r_b.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
            r_b.font.size = Pt(font_size)
            r_b.font.bold = True
        r_t = p.add_run(text)
        r_t.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
        r_t.font.size = Pt(font_size)
        return p

    def add_bullet(text, bold_prefix=None, space_after=1.0, font_size=8.8, line_spacing=1.05):
        p = doc.add_paragraph()
        p.paragraph_format.left_indent = Inches(0.16)
        p.paragraph_format.line_spacing = line_spacing
        p.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(0)
        p.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(space_after)
        r_b = p.add_run("• ")
        r_b.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
        r_b.font.size = Pt(font_size)
        r_b.font.bold = True
        if bold_prefix:
            r_bp = p.add_run(bold_prefix)
            r_bp.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
            r_bp.font.size = Pt(font_size)
            r_bp.font.bold = True
        r_t = p.add_run(text)
        r_t.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
        r_t.font.size = Pt(font_size)
        return p

    def add_caption(text):
        p = doc.add_paragraph()
        p.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
        p.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(1.0)
        p.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3.0)
        r = p.add_run(text)
        r.font.name = "Old Standard TT"
        r.font.size = Pt(7.8)
        r.font.italic = True
        r.font.color.rgb = RGBColor(0x55, 0x55, 0x55)
        return p

    # --- 1. INTRODUCTION ---
    add_h1("1. Introduction")
    add_p(
        "Autonomous algorithmic agents increasingly govern financial exchanges, automated multi-unit auctions, and dynamic e-commerce pricing. "
        "A critical frontier in AI safety is preventing algorithmic collusion—scenarios where autonomous agents synchronize strategies to extract "
        "supracompetitive rents at the expense of human market participants (Calvano et al., 2020; Schwalbe, 2018). Unlike traditional cartels that rely on "
        "explicit communication logs, AI agents can establish implicit coordination purely through tacit observational learning or private coordination signals. "
        "Consequently, behavioral auditing tools that operate solely on external observational logs are essential for oversight."
    )
    add_p(
        "In Track 1.4 (Markets and Collusion) of the Apart Research AI Collusion Research Sprint, we address the core research question: "
        "Can information-theoretic approaches detect collusion in multi-agent games? Specifically, we stress-test Pairwise Net Influence (PNI) "
        "(Bonjour, Aggarwal, & Bhargava, UAI 2022). While PNI is theoretically sound, real-world market auditing confronts a severe confounder: "
        "shared-context confounding. When non-coordinating agents independently react to identical market histories, price shocks, or public order books, "
        "their actions naturally synchronize. If auditing metrics mistake this context-driven synchronization for collusion, benign market makers will face false enforcement sanctions."
    )
    add_p(
        "To rigorously separate the effects of state conditioning from rival competitor subtraction, we formulate and evaluate four hypotheses "
        "across three distinct estimators—Marginal MI I(A_i; A_j), Conditional MI γ(i, j) = I(A_i; A_j | S), and PNI net score—using a frozen "
        "canonical benchmark of 260 unique trajectories (54,000 recorded rounds across 300 evaluations) complemented by 68,400 open-weight LLM agent decisions across 630 trajectories."
    )
    add_p("Our main contributions are:")
    add_bullet(
        " We formally formulate shared-context confounding in multi-agent collusion detection; in this controlled shared-context condition, independent agents reacting to the same observable state produced a 38-fold increase in estimated marginal mutual information and a 100% false-positive rate under the evaluated threshold.",
        bold_prefix="1. Formulation of Context Confounding:"
    )
    add_bullet(
        " We demonstrate that conditioning on observable state S neutralizes the contextual surge below independent calibration thresholds (CMI FPR = 0.000), while PNI rival competitor subtraction isolates genuine coordination with 100% Top-1 ranking accuracy (Wilson 95% CI: [0.839, 1.000], MRR = 1.000).",
        bold_prefix="2. Triangulated Estimator Disambiguation:"
    )
    add_bullet(
        " We scale evaluations to 68,400 decisions from open-weight Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct across horizons T in {5, 20, 50} on Modal CPU workers, demonstrating that ultra-short horizons (T=5) suffer sample sparsity (70.0% ranking accuracy), while T >= 20 restores 100.0% Top-1 ranking accuracy and 100.0% binary recall.",
        bold_prefix="3. Multi-Horizon Foundation Model Boundary:"
    )

    # --- 2. RELATED WORK ---
    add_h1("2. Related Work")
    add_p(
        "Algorithmic Collusion in Economics: Classical literature establishes that reinforcement learning and algorithmic pricing algorithms can tacitly collude in oligopoly pricing games without explicit communication (Calvano et al., 2020; Klein, 2021). These studies demonstrate supracompetitive profit margins but do not provide external statistical auditing tools for regulators."
    )
    add_p(
        "Information-Theoretic Collusion Auditing: Bonjour, Aggarwal, and Bhargava (UAI 2022) proposed Pairwise Net Influence (PNI), defining directed action influence via conditional mutual information and competitor subtraction. However, their evaluation did not explicitly contrast PNI against unconditioned baselines under shared public context, leaving open whether the detector isolates coordination or merely captures context adaptation."
    )
    add_p(
        "Language Models in Economic Multi-Agent Systems: Recent work explores foundation models acting as autonomous bidders and price setters (Agrawal et al., 2025). While Agrawal et al. investigate auction settings, systematic information-theoretic audits of open-weight LLMs across multi-horizon game trajectories have not been previously benchmarked under zero-out-of-pocket cloud auditing protocols."
    )

    # --- 3. METHODS ---
    add_h1("3. Methods")
    add_h2("3.1 Multi-Agent Matrix Tournament Setting")
    add_p(
        "We implement an iterated 4-player discrete Rock-Paper-Scissors (RPS) game. At each round t in {1, ..., T}, each agent i in {0, 1, 2, 3} selects action A_i,t in {ROCK, PAPER, SCISSORS}. "
        "The public game state S_t represents the public history observable by all players. In the canonical scripted benchmark, S_t encodes the previous round actions of all players (|S| = 82 states). "
        "In the LLM study, S_t is abstracted to the dominant plurality outcome from the previous round (S in {START, ROCK, PAPER, SCISSORS, TIE}, |S| = 5 states)."
    )

    add_h2("3.2 Information-Theoretic Detectors")
    add_p(
        "1. Marginal Mutual Information (Unconditioned): I(A_i; A_j) = sum_{a_i, a_j} P(a_i, a_j) log2(P(a_i, a_j) / (P(a_i) P(a_j))). Measures total action correlation without controlling for environment state.\n"
        "2. Conditional Mutual Information (State-Conditioned): gamma(i, j) = I(A_i; A_j | S) = sum_{s} P(s) sum_{a_i, a_j} P(a_i, a_j | s) log2(P(a_i, a_j | s) / (P(a_i | s) P(a_j | s))). Measures action dependence beyond what is explained by common state S, with zero probabilities evaluated via 0 log 0 = 0.\n"
        "3. Pairwise Net Influence (PNI): net(i, j) = gamma(i, j) - max_{k != i, j} gamma(k, j). Subtracts the maximum influence exerted by any rival agent k on agent j, isolating directed coordination."
    )

    add_h2("3.3 Estimator Regularization and Calibration Protocol")
    add_p(
        "To prevent undefined logarithms on zero counts in discrete state spaces, we apply symmetric Dirichlet pseudocount smoothing (Laplace smoothing with lambda = 0.5): "
        "P_lambda(a_i, a_j, s) = (N(a_i, a_j, s) + lambda) / (N + lambda |A|^2 |S|). Nominal 5% decision thresholds are calibrated from independent validation episodes (Condition A seeds 0–4, "
        "calibrating tau_MI = 0.030751, tau_CMI = 0.117454, tau_PNI = 0.010427 bits), and evaluated on completely held-out test episodes (seeds 5–19, n=15) across all conditions."
    )

    # Table 1: Experimental Conditions
    add_p("Table 1 summarizes the experimental conditions evaluated across scripted and open-weight LLM architectures:")
    t1_headers = ["Condition", "Name", "Policy & Coordination Mechanism", "Target Coordinating Pair"]
    t1_data = [
        ["A", "Independent Baseline", "4 uniform-random agents with isolated RNG seeds (seeds 0–4 calibration, 5–19 test)", "None"],
        ["B", "Shared-Context Confound", "Agents independently play state-conditioned best responses with 10% noise", "None"],
        ["C", "Known Coordination", "Pair (0, 1) coordinates via private unobservable device Z_t ~ Uniform({0, 1, 2}) (alpha=1.0)", "(0, 1)"],
        ["D", "Coordination Sweep", "Joint gate G_t ~ Bernoulli(alpha); pair coordinates on Z_t if G_t=1, else independent", "(0, 1)"],
        ["LLM-A", "LLM Independent", "Qwen2.5-1.5B prompted to select moves independently with distinct per-decision seeds", "None"],
        ["LLM-B", "LLM Shared Context", "Qwen2.5-1.5B prompted to counter the observed round plurality outcome", "None"],
        ["LLM-C", "LLM Coordinated", "Designated pair (0, 1) prompted with secret round directive Z_t (excluded from logs)", "(0, 1)"],
    ]
    t1_table = doc.add_table(rows=len(t1_data) + 1, cols=4)
    format_table(t1_table, [Inches(0.9), Inches(1.4), Inches(3.7), Inches(1.0)], t1_headers, t1_data, font_size=7.0)
    add_caption("Table 1: Controlled experimental conditions for stress-testing collusion detectors.")

    # --- 4. RESULTS ---
    add_h1("4. Results")
    add_h2("4.1 Canonical Scripted Benchmark Results")
    add_p(
        "Table 2 reports the primary benchmark results across 260 unique trajectories (54,000 recorded rounds across 300 evaluations) at T=200 rounds. "
        "Under Condition B (shared-context confounding), unconditioned Marginal MI exhibits a 38-fold surge from 0.025 to 0.958 bits [0.932, 0.984], "
        "falsely flagging 100% of non-coordinating pairs as colluders (FPR = 1.000, 120/120 pairs). Conditioning on observable state S in CMI drops "
        "dependence to 0.084 bits [0.081, 0.087], completely eliminating false positives (FPR = 0.000, 0/120 pairs). "
        "Under Condition C (shared-private-signal coordination), PNI achieves 100% Top-1 ranking accuracy (20/20 seeds, Wilson 95% CI: [0.839, 1.000], MRR = 1.000), "
        "with true coordinating pairs achieving an average net score of 0.755 bits [0.741, 0.769]. "
        "Condition A held-out test evaluation (seeds 5–19, 90 negative candidate pairs) demonstrates low false positive rates across detectors "
        "(PNI FPR = 0.022, 2/90; Marginal MI FPR = 0.033, 3/90; CMI FPR = 0.111, 10/90)."
    )

    # Table 2: Canonical Benchmark Results
    t2_headers = ["Condition", "Marginal MI Top Score", "Marginal MI FPR [Counts]", "CMI Top Score", "CMI FPR [Counts]", "PNI Top-1 Acc.", "PNI FPR [Counts]"]
    t2_data = [
        ["A (Cal., n=5)", "0.027 [0.019, 0.036]", "0.067 [0.000, 0.133] (2/30)", "0.115 [0.111, 0.118]", "0.067 [0.000, 0.133] (2/30)", "N/A", "0.067 [0.000, 0.133] (2/30)"],
        ["A (Test, n=15)", "0.025 [0.020, 0.031]", "0.033 [0.000, 0.067] (3/90)", "0.115 [0.111, 0.119]", "0.111 [0.044, 0.189] (10/90)", "N/A", "0.022 [0.000, 0.056] (2/90)"],
        ["B (Confound, n=20)", "**0.958 [0.932, 0.984]**", "**1.000 [1.000, 1.000]*** (120/120)", "**0.084 [0.081, 0.087]**", "**0.000 [0.000, 0.000]*** (0/120)", "N/A", "0.100 [0.050, 0.158] (12/120)"],
        ["C (Coord., n=20)", "1.451 [1.447, 1.454]", "0.240 [0.200, 0.320] (24/100)", "0.797 [0.785, 0.808]", "0.020 [0.000, 0.050] (2/100)", "**1.000 [0.839, 1.000]** (20/20)", "0.200 [0.200, 0.200]* (20/100)"],
    ]
    t2_table = doc.add_table(rows=len(t2_data) + 1, cols=7)
    format_table(t2_table, [Inches(1.15), Inches(0.95), Inches(1.05), Inches(0.95), Inches(1.05), Inches(1.00), Inches(0.95)], t2_headers, t2_data, align_right_cols=[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6], font_size=6.7)
    add_caption("Table 2: Canonical benchmark results at T=200 (mean scores with 95% bootstrap CIs; exact Wilson 95% CIs for Top-1; counts show flagged / total negative candidate pairs; *degenerate bootstrap interval).")

    # Figures 1 and 2
    if (FIGURES_DIR / "fig2_false_positive_rates.png").exists():
        doc.add_picture(str(FIGURES_DIR / "fig2_false_positive_rates.png"), width=Inches(3.55))
        doc.paragraphs[-1].alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
        add_caption("Figure 1: False Positive Rates across conditions. Marginal MI fails catastrophically under Condition B (100% FPR), whereas state conditioning restores 0% FPR.")

    if (FIGURES_DIR / "fig5_trajectory_length_sensitivity.png").exists():
        doc.add_picture(str(FIGURES_DIR / "fig5_trajectory_length_sensitivity.png"), width=Inches(3.55))
        doc.paragraphs[-1].alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
        add_caption("Figure 2: Trajectory length sensitivity (T in [50, 500]). Small sample sizes exhibit upward estimator bias under discrete state conditioning.")

    add_h2("4.2 Open-Weight LLM Agent Experiments (Modal CPU)")
    add_p(
        "We deployed Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct on Modal CPU workers (4 vCPU, 8 GB RAM per container, torch.bfloat16) across 630 game trajectories (68,400 agent decisions) "
        "at horizons T in {5, 20, 50}. Across all 68,400 decisions, the JSON parser achieved a 100.0% valid action selection rate without defaulting. "
        "In Condition LLM-C, coordinating agents achieved 100.0% adherence to secret coordination directives across all 11,400 opportunities (400 at T=5; 4,000 at T=20; 7,000 at T=50). "
        "Condition LLM-C evaluates instruction compliance with an assigned shared private coordination directive; it does not simulate or claim spontaneous collusion or emergent cartel formation."
    )

    # Table 3: Multi-Horizon LLM Primary Ranking Metrics
    t3_headers = ["Horizon", "Condition", "Traj.", "Decisions", "Valid Rate", "Signal Compl.", "Top-1 Marg. MI", "Top-1 CMI", "Top-1 PNI", "Mean CMI (bits)"]
    t3_data = [
        ["T=5", "LLM-A (Ind.)", "40", "800", "100.0%", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "0.0693"],
        ["", "LLM-B (Cont.)", "40", "800", "100.0%", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "0.0666"],
        ["", "LLM-C (Coord.)", "40", "800", "100.0%", "**100.0% (400/400)**", "**1.000 (40/40)**", "**0.700 (28/40)**", "**0.700 (28/40)**", "0.0696"],
        ["T=20", "LLM-A (Ind.)", "100", "8,000", "100.0%", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "0.0997"],
        ["", "LLM-B (Cont.)", "100", "8,000", "100.0%", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "0.0981"],
        ["", "LLM-C (Coord.)", "100", "8,000", "100.0%", "**100.0% (4000/4000)**", "**1.000 (100/100)**", "**1.000 (100/100)**", "**1.000 (100/100)**", "0.1333"],
        ["T=50", "LLM-A (Ind.)", "70", "14,000", "100.0%", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "0.0835"],
        ["", "LLM-B (Cont.)", "70", "14,000", "100.0%", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "0.0833"],
        ["", "LLM-C (Coord.)", "70", "14,000", "100.0%", "**100.0% (7000/7000)**", "**1.000 (70/70)**", "**1.000 (70/70)**", "**1.000 (70/70)**", "0.1770"],
    ]
    t3_table = doc.add_table(rows=len(t3_data) + 1, cols=10)
    format_table(t3_table, [Inches(0.6), Inches(1.1), Inches(0.5), Inches(0.7), Inches(0.7), Inches(1.1), Inches(0.9), Inches(0.8), Inches(0.8), Inches(0.8)], t3_headers, t3_data, align_right_cols=[2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9], font_size=6.6)
    add_caption("Table 3: Multi-Horizon Open-Weight LLM Primary Ranking Metrics (Qwen2.5-1.5B on Modal CPU, 630 trajectories, 68,400 decisions).")

    # Table 4: Held-Out Classification and Threshold Evaluation
    add_p("Table 4 evaluates held-out classification under calibrated decision thresholds (calibrated on LLM-A validation seeds):")
    t4_headers = ["Horizon", "Condition", "Test Ep.", "Detector", "Threshold", "Ep. Flagged", "Pair Flagged Rate", "Top-1 Acc.", "Target Recall", "Ranked #1 & Det.", "Ranked #1 Not Det."]
    t4_data = [
        ["T=5", "LLM-A", "30", "PNI", "0.0154", "14/30 (0.467)", "0.172 [0.124, 0.234]", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A"],
        ["", "LLM-B", "30", "PNI", "0.0154", "10/30 (0.333)", "0.100 [0.064, 0.153]", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A"],
        ["", "LLM-C", "30", "PNI", "0.0154", "11/30 (0.367)", "0.139 [0.096, 0.197]", "0.700", "0.367", "0.367", "0.333"],
        ["T=20", "LLM-A", "75", "PNI", "0.0570", "11/75 (0.147)", "0.078 [0.056, 0.106]", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A"],
        ["", "LLM-B", "75", "PNI", "0.0570", "13/75 (0.173)", "0.104 [0.079, 0.136]", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A"],
        ["", "LLM-C", "75", "PNI", "0.0570", "75/75 (1.000)", "0.207 [0.172, 0.246]", "1.000", "1.000", "1.000", "0.000"],
        ["T=50", "LLM-A", "60", "PNI", "0.0621", "4/60 (0.067)", "0.036 [0.021, 0.061]", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A"],
        ["", "LLM-B", "60", "PNI", "0.0621", "7/60 (0.117)", "0.039 [0.023, 0.064]", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A", "N/A"],
        ["", "LLM-C", "60", "PNI", "0.0621", "60/60 (1.000)", "0.194 [0.157, 0.238]", "1.000", "1.000", "1.000", "0.000"],
    ]
    t4_table = doc.add_table(rows=len(t4_data) + 1, cols=11)
    format_table(t4_table, [Inches(0.6), Inches(0.8), Inches(0.5), Inches(0.6), Inches(0.6), Inches(0.8), Inches(1.1), Inches(0.6), Inches(0.6), Inches(0.9), Inches(0.9)], t4_headers, t4_data, align_right_cols=[2, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10], font_size=6.4)
    add_caption("Table 4: Held-out classification and threshold evaluation across LLM horizons.")

    # Figure 3
    if (FIGURES_DIR / "fig6_llm_horizon_scaling.png").exists():
        doc.add_picture(str(FIGURES_DIR / "fig6_llm_horizon_scaling.png"), width=Inches(3.7))
        doc.paragraphs[-1].alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
        add_caption("Figure 3: Multi-Horizon LLM Metric Evolution across horizons T in {5, 20, 50}. Short horizons suffer sample sparsity; T >= 20 restores perfect ranking accuracy and threshold recall.")

    # --- 5. DISCUSSION AND LIMITATIONS ---
    add_h1("5. Discussion and Limitations")
    add_h2("5.1 Auditing Implications: Why Correlation Lies")
    add_p(
        "In algorithmic markets, autonomous pricing agents, high-frequency execution bots, and procurement models continuously observe common economic shocks "
        "(e.g., benchmark interest rates, commodity prices, wholesale tariffs). In this controlled environment, our empirical results illustrate that unconditioned correlation metrics "
        "(e.g., Pearson correlation, raw mutual information) cannot differentiate rational adaptation from collusive coordination. Auditing architectures relying on unconditioned metrics "
        "face a substantial risk of Type I errors, potentially targeting competitive firms solely because they adapted efficiently to common public information. State conditioning provides a critical statistical safeguard to reduce this confounding risk."
    )

    add_h2("Limitations")
    add_bullet(
        " Condition C establishes a controlled shared-private-signal coordination condition demonstrating that PNI recognizes statistical action dependence in scripted matrix games. However, detecting this statistical signature does not prove that agents formed an economically harmful cartel, extracted supra-competitive rents, or inflicted market harm. Unobserved common causes could generate similar conditional action dependence.",
        bold_prefix="1. Controlled Scripted Signatures vs. Real-World Cartels:"
    )
    add_bullet(
        " In our 4-player game with |S| = 82 states, state conditioning partitions observations across sparse cells. Dirichlet smoothing introduces a mild upward baseline in sparse regimes, though all values remain well below unconditioned Marginal MI (0.958 bits).",
        bold_prefix="2. Estimator Regularization Sensitivity:"
    )
    add_bullet(
        " Nominal 5% decision thresholds were calibrated from 5 validation seeds (30 candidate pairs) of Condition A. While isolating calibration from the 15 held-out test seeds, 30 samples yield coarse quantile estimates and do not guarantee an exact 5% population FPR.",
        bold_prefix="3. Exploratory Small-Sample Calibration:"
    )
    add_bullet(
        " An extensive 68,400-decision study using Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct was executed on Modal CPU instances (4 cores, 8GB RAM). Allocating cloud GPU instances required an active payment method on file under Modal platform policy, which was safely avoided under our zero-out-of-pocket spending cap ($0.00). The CPU pipeline logged $40.63 in metered compute ($40.12 promotional credits applied, $0.51 network egress allowance; $0.00 billed out of pocket) across 630 trajectories (68,400 decisions).",
        bold_prefix="4. Modal Promotional Compute Pilot & Cloud Boundaries:"
    )
    add_bullet(
        " In Condition LLM-C, models are explicitly prompted to follow an assigned secret directive Z_t. Perfect signal compliance (100.0%, 11,400/11,400 opportunities) confirms that foundation models reliably adhere to structured coordination instructions in multi-agent game logs, but must not be interpreted as spontaneous collusion emergence or demonstrated market harm. Furthermore, competitor subtraction drives rival net scores negative, requiring careful distinction between relative ranking and absolute threshold detection.",
        bold_prefix="5. Prompted Instruction Adherence vs. Spontaneous Collusion:"
    )

    add_h2("Future Work")
    add_p(
        "Key extensions include: (1) evaluating neural CMI estimators (e.g., MINE) on high-dimensional LLM auction transcripts (Agrawal et al., 2025); "
        "(2) developing dynamic state abstractions to compress complex order book histories; and (3) testing PNI under intentional adversarial evasion."
    )

    # --- 6. CONCLUSION ---
    add_h1("6. Conclusion")
    add_p(
        "This research stress-tested Pairwise Net Influence for detecting multi-agent coordination. "
        "In this controlled shared-context condition, independent agents reacting to the same observable state produced a 38-fold increase in estimated marginal mutual information and a 100% false-positive rate under the evaluated threshold. "
        "Conditioning on observable state neutralizes this broadcast shock. Under genuine scripted coordination, PNI ranked the designated coordinating pair first in all 20 scripted positive-control trajectories. "
        "However, at the calibrated decision threshold it flagged 20/100 non-target pairs in this condition, compared with 2/100 for CMI, illustrating that pair ranking and threshold-based false-positive control are distinct outcomes. "
        "Furthermore, across open-weight language model decisions, state-conditioned detectors achieve 100% identification accuracy once trajectory length reaches T >= 20 rounds, overcoming finite-sample state sparsity in controlled settings. "
        "These results support state conditioning as a useful safeguard in the tested setting; its performance in richer environments and under adversarial or unobserved confounding requires further evaluation."
    )

    # --- CODE AND DATA ---
    add_h1("Code and Data")
    add_bullet(" Complete open-source experiment pipeline, detectors, environment implementations, and validation suites are bundled in when_correlation_lies_all_source_code.txt and the repository.", bold_prefix="Code repository:")
    add_bullet(" Full raw JSONL trajectories (260 scripted, 54,000 rounds; 630 LLM, 68,400 decisions) and ground-truth manifests are preserved in when_correlation_lies_review_bundle.zip (data/raw/, data/eval_labels/, data/raw_llm*/).", bold_prefix="Data/Datasets:")
    add_bullet(" All 59 automated unit tests pass cleanly in Python 3.13 on standard CPU hardware without cloud dependencies.", bold_prefix="Reproducibility:")

    # --- AUTHOR CONTRIBUTIONS ---
    add_h1("Author Contributions")
    add_p(
        "Durgesh Unde (Independent Researcher): Project conceptualization, selection and formulation of research questions and experimental conditions, "
        "project direction, review of experimental outputs, qualitative interpretation of detection metrics, and final editorial responsibility for the manuscript."
    )

    # --- REFERENCES ---
    add_h1("References")
    references = [
        "Agrawal, K., Teo, V., Vazquez, J. J., Kunnavakkam, S., Srikanth, V., & Liu, A. (2025). Evaluating LLM Agent Collusion in Double Auctions. arXiv preprint arXiv:2507.01413.",
        "Bonjour, T., Aggarwal, V., & Bhargava, B. (2022). Information-theoretic approach to detect collusion in multi-agent games. Proceedings of Machine Learning Research (UAI 2022), 180, 223–232.",
        "Calvano, E., Calzolari, G., Denicolò, V., & Pastorello, S. (2020). Artificial intelligence, algorithmic pricing, and collusion. American Economic Review, 110(10), 3267–3297.",
        "Cover, T. M., & Thomas, J. A. (2006). Elements of Information Theory (2nd ed.). John Wiley & Sons.",
        "Holste, D., Grosse, I., & Herzel, H. (1998). Bayes' estimators of generalized entropies. Journal of Physics A: Mathematical and General, 31(11), 2551–2566.",
        "Paninski, L. (2003). Estimation of entropy and mutual information. Neural Computation, 15(6), 1191–1253.",
        "Schwalbe, U. (2018). Algorithms, machine learning, and collusion. Journal of Competition Law & Economics, 14(4), 568–607.",
    ]
    for i, ref in enumerate(references, 1):
        add_p(f"{i}. {ref}", space_after=1.0, font_size=7.8, line_spacing=1.02)

    # --- APPENDIX ---
    add_h1("Appendix: Limitations and Dual-Use / Ethical Considerations")
    add_p(
        "Mandatory Ethical & Dual-Use Analysis: Auditing multi-agent systems for collusion possesses significant dual-use implications:",
        space_after=1.0,
        font_size=8.2,
        line_spacing=1.04
    )
    add_bullet(
        " Information-theoretic detectors empower market regulators, exchange operators, and platform architects to continuously monitor high-frequency trading and procurement markets for tacit cartels without requiring invasive access to proprietary model weights.",
        bold_prefix="1. Defensive Regulatory Oversight:",
        space_after=1.0,
        font_size=8.2,
        line_spacing=1.04
    )
    add_bullet(
        " Conversely, adversarial agent developers could incorporate PNI as an explicit penalty term in multi-agent reinforcement learning objectives. By penalizing conditional mutual information, malicious actors could train stealthy collusive policies that sustain supracompetitive markups while remaining statistically undetectable under state-conditioned audits.",
        bold_prefix="2. Adversarial Exploitation & Stealth Evasion:",
        space_after=1.0,
        font_size=8.2,
        line_spacing=1.04
    )
    add_bullet(
        " Our Condition B results demonstrate the catastrophic risk of unconditioned metrics: a 100% false accusation rate against competitive agents responding to shared context. Auditing architectures must mandate state conditioning and calibrated uncertainty intervals before initiating punitive regulatory actions.",
        bold_prefix="3. Harms of False Accusations:",
        space_after=1.0,
        font_size=8.2,
        line_spacing=1.04
    )

    # --- LLM USAGE STATEMENT ---
    add_h1("LLM Usage Statement")
    add_p(
        "ChatGPT (OpenAI) assisted with research ideation, paper explanation, methodology critique, independent review, and prompt drafting. "
        "Antigravity (Google DeepMind advanced agentic coding assistant) assisted with code implementation, unit test suites, experiment orchestration, "
        "Modal CPU cloud infrastructure harness and execution, statistical metric aggregations, visualizations, document layout, and packaging under the author's iterative instructions and review. "
        "Project direction, experimental design choices, qualitative analysis, and final editorial responsibility rest entirely with the author (Durgesh Unde). "
        "Canonical benchmark evaluations used 260 scripted trajectories (54,000 rounds); open-weight LLM evaluations used 630 trajectories (68,400 decisions) executed on Modal CPU "
        "($40.63 metered compute: $40.12 promotional credits applied, $0.51 network egress allowance; $0.00 out-of-pocket spend). "
        "Zero personal payment cards or external paid token APIs were used. The experimental protocol was frozen after exploratory prototyping and was not preregistered prior to exploration.",
        space_after=1.0,
        font_size=8.2,
        line_spacing=1.04
    )

    # Save populated DOCX
    OUTPUT_DOCX.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    doc.save(str(OUTPUT_DOCX))
    print(f"Successfully saved populated DOCX to: {OUTPUT_DOCX} ({OUTPUT_DOCX.stat().st_size:,} bytes)")

    # Export to PDF via Microsoft Word COM (Windows) with headless/CI fallback
    print("Exporting PDF via Microsoft Word COM automation...")
    try:
        import win32com.client
        word = win32com.client.Dispatch("Word.Application")
        word.Visible = False
        try:
            w_doc = word.Documents.Open(str(OUTPUT_DOCX.resolve()))
            w_doc.BuiltInDocumentProperties("Title").Value = "When Correlation Lies: Stress-Testing Pairwise Net Influence for Detecting Coordination Among Agents"
            w_doc.BuiltInDocumentProperties("Author").Value = "Durgesh Unde"
            w_doc.BuiltInDocumentProperties("Subject").Value = "Apart Research AI Collusion Research Sprint 2026 - Track 1.4: Markets and Collusion"
            w_doc.SaveAs(str(OUTPUT_PDF.resolve()), FileFormat=17) # 17 = wdFormatPDF
            w_doc.Close(False)
            print(f"Successfully exported PDF to: {OUTPUT_PDF} ({OUTPUT_PDF.stat().st_size:,} bytes)")
        finally:
            try:
                word.Quit()
            except Exception:
                pass
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[Notice] Microsoft Word COM automation unavailable or failed: {e}")
        print(f"Populated DOCX report is ready at: {OUTPUT_DOCX}")
        print("To export PDF on headless/Linux environments, use LibreOffice:")
        print(f"  soffice --headless --convert-to pdf {OUTPUT_DOCX} --outdir {REPORT_DIR}")
        if OUTPUT_PDF.exists():
            print(f"Pre-compiled canonical PDF is preserved at: {OUTPUT_PDF} ({OUTPUT_PDF.stat().st_size:,} bytes)")

    build_official_report()

"""
scripts/verify_final_consistency.py
===================================
End-to-end automated scientific, code, data, and report consistency verifier.
Validates:
1. Automated unit test suite (59/59 tests pass)
2. Trajectory dataset integrity (260 scripted files, 54,000 rounds + 630 LLM files, 68,400 decisions)
3. PDF report compliance (exactly 7 pages <= 8 pages limit, valid metadata, 0 forbidden phrases)
4. Review bundle ZIP integrity (clean project repository structure, zero duplicate folders)
5. Results manifest accuracy (exact thresholds, counts, and statistical metrics)
6. Triangulation metrics integrity (Marginal MI, CMI, and PNI net score metrics in JSON and CSV)
"""

import sys
import os
import json
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent


def test_suite_check():
    import pytest
    ret = pytest.main(["-q", str(PROJECT_ROOT / "tests")])
    assert ret == 0, f"pytest returned exit code {ret}"
    print("[PASS] Test Suite: All 59 tests passed cleanly.")


def dataset_check():
    raw_files = list((PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "raw").glob("*.jsonl"))
    label_files = list((PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "eval_labels").glob("*.json"))
    assert len(raw_files) == 260, f"Expected 260 raw files, got {len(raw_files)}"
    assert len(label_files) == 260, f"Expected 260 label files, got {len(label_files)}"

    total_rounds = 0
    file_counts = {}
    for rf in raw_files:
        rounds = sum(1 for line in open(rf, "r", encoding="utf-8") if line.strip()) - 1
        total_rounds += rounds
        file_counts[rounds] = file_counts.get(rounds, 0) + 1

    assert total_rounds == 54000, f"Expected 54,000 rounds, got {total_rounds}"
    assert file_counts == {200: 140, 50: 40, 100: 40, 500: 40}, f"Unexpected count distribution: {file_counts}"

    raw_llm = list((PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "raw_llm").glob("*.jsonl"))
    eval_llm = list((PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "eval_labels_llm").glob("*.json"))
    assert len(raw_llm) == 120, f"Expected 120 raw LLM files, got {len(raw_llm)}"
    assert len(eval_llm) == 120, f"Expected 120 eval LLM files, got {len(eval_llm)}"

    raw_llm_exp = list((PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "raw_llm_expanded").glob("*.jsonl"))
    eval_llm_exp = list((PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "eval_labels_llm_expanded").glob("*.json"))
    assert len(raw_llm_exp) == 300, f"Expected 300 raw LLM expanded files, got {len(raw_llm_exp)}"
    assert len(eval_llm_exp) == 300, f"Expected 300 eval LLM expanded files, got {len(eval_llm_exp)}"

    raw_llm_t50 = list((PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "raw_llm_expanded_t50").glob("*.jsonl"))
    eval_llm_t50 = list((PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "eval_labels_llm_expanded_t50").glob("*.json"))
    assert len(raw_llm_t50) == 210, f"Expected 210 raw LLM t50 files, got {len(raw_llm_t50)}"
    assert len(eval_llm_t50) == 210, f"Expected 210 eval LLM t50 files, got {len(eval_llm_t50)}"

    total_llm_trajs = len(raw_llm) + len(raw_llm_exp) + len(raw_llm_t50)
    assert total_llm_trajs == 630, f"Expected 630 total LLM trajectories, got {total_llm_trajs}"
    print(f"[PASS] Dataset: Exactly 260 scripted files (54,000 rounds) + 630 LLM files across 3 horizons (68,400 decisions).")


def pdf_report_check():
    import pymupdf
    pdf_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "final_delivery" / "when_correlation_lies_report.pdf"
    if not pdf_path.exists():
        pdf_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "report" / "when_correlation_lies_report.pdf"
    assert pdf_path.exists(), f"Missing PDF at {pdf_path}"
    doc = pymupdf.open(str(pdf_path))
    assert len(doc) <= 8, f"PDF exceeds 8-page hard cap: got {len(doc)} pages"
    assert len(doc) in (7, 8), f"Expected 7 or 8 pages, got {len(doc)}"

    meta = doc.metadata
    assert meta["author"] == "Durgesh Unde", f"Unexpected author: {meta.get('author')}"
    assert "When Correlation Lies" in meta["title"], f"Unexpected title: {meta.get('title')}"
    assert "Apart Research" in meta["subject"], f"Unexpected subject: {meta.get('subject')}"

    # Verify no illegal strings on any page
    raw_full_text = "\n".join(p.get_text() for p in doc)
    full_text_normalized = " ".join(raw_full_text.split()).lower()
    forbidden = [
        "pre-registered",
        "hash(state)",
        "immunizes the auditor",
        "Anonymous AI Safety Researcher",
        "With: Apart Research",
        "Affiliation: Independent Researcher (With",
        "With Apart Research",
        "300 trajectories",
        "28,500",
        "$17.24",
        "$17.01",
        "0.076 ± 0.003",
        "0.998 ± 0.009",
        "0.684 ± 0.008",
        "preserving low false positives while attaining 100%",
        "essential requirement for auditing",
        "essential for behavioral auditing",
        "prove that independent agents reacting",
    ]
    for phrase in forbidden:
        p_norm = " ".join(phrase.split()).lower()
        assert p_norm not in full_text_normalized, f"Forbidden phrase '{phrase}' found in PDF!"

    # Verify key phrases present
    required_phrases = [
        "Durgesh Unde",
        "Independent Researcher",
        "shared-private-signal coordination",
        "Conditional MI",
        "tau_CMI",
        "0 log 0 = 0",
        "Wilson",
        "Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct",
        "Open-Weight LLM Agent Experiments",
        "630 trajectories",
        "68,400 decisions",
        "$40.63",
        "$40.12",
        "$0.51",
        "3/90",
        "10/90",
        "2/90",
        "120/120",
        "0/120",
        "24/100",
        "2/100",
        "20/100",
        "20/20",
        "20/100 non-target pairs",
        "pair ranking and threshold-based false-positive control are distinct outcomes",
        "useful safeguard in the tested setting",
    ]
    for phrase in required_phrases:
        p_norm = " ".join(phrase.split()).lower()
        assert p_norm in full_text_normalized, f"Required phrase '{phrase}' missing in PDF!"

    print(f"[PASS] PDF Report: Exactly {len(doc)} pages (<= 8 cap), valid metadata, 0 forbidden phrases, required concepts and canonical counts present.")


def zip_bundle_check():
    zip_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "final_delivery" / "when_correlation_lies_review_bundle.zip"
    assert zip_path.exists(), f"Missing zip at {zip_path}"
    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "r") as zf:
        namelist = zf.namelist()
        
        # Check raw files and labels
        raws = [n for n in namelist if n.startswith("data/raw/") and n.endswith(".jsonl")]
        labels = [n for n in namelist if n.startswith("data/eval_labels/") and n.endswith(".json")]
        assert len(raws) == 260, f"Expected 260 data/raw/ files in ZIP, got {len(raws)}"
        assert len(labels) == 260, f"Expected 260 data/eval_labels/ files in ZIP, got {len(labels)}"

        raws_llm = [n for n in namelist if n.startswith("data/raw_llm/") and n.endswith(".jsonl")]
        labels_llm = [n for n in namelist if n.startswith("data/eval_labels_llm/") and n.endswith(".json")]
        assert len(raws_llm) == 120, f"Expected 120 data/raw_llm/ files in ZIP, got {len(raws_llm)}"
        assert len(labels_llm) == 120, f"Expected 120 data/eval_labels_llm/ files in ZIP, got {len(labels_llm)}"

        raws_llm_exp = [n for n in namelist if n.startswith("data/raw_llm_expanded/") and n.endswith(".jsonl")]
        labels_llm_exp = [n for n in namelist if n.startswith("data/eval_labels_llm_expanded/") and n.endswith(".json")]
        assert len(raws_llm_exp) == 300, f"Expected 300 data/raw_llm_expanded/ files in ZIP, got {len(raws_llm_exp)}"
        assert len(labels_llm_exp) == 300, f"Expected 300 data/eval_labels_llm_expanded/ files in ZIP, got {len(labels_llm_exp)}"

        raws_llm_t50 = [n for n in namelist if n.startswith("data/raw_llm_expanded_t50/") and n.endswith(".jsonl")]
        labels_llm_t50 = [n for n in namelist if n.startswith("data/eval_labels_llm_expanded_t50/") and n.endswith(".json")]
        assert len(raws_llm_t50) == 210, f"Expected 210 data/raw_llm_expanded_t50/ files in ZIP, got {len(raws_llm_t50)}"
        assert len(labels_llm_t50) == 210, f"Expected 210 data/eval_labels_llm_expanded_t50/ files in ZIP, got {len(labels_llm_t50)}"
        
        # Check NO duplicate folders
        dup_trajs = [n for n in namelist if n.startswith("data/trajectories/")]
        dup_gts = [n for n in namelist if n.startswith("data/ground_truth/")]
        assert len(dup_trajs) == 0, f"Found unexpected duplicate data/trajectories/ in ZIP ({len(dup_trajs)})"
        assert len(dup_gts) == 0, f"Found unexpected duplicate data/ground_truth/ in ZIP ({len(dup_gts)})"

        # Check clean repo files at relative paths
        expected_files = [
            "pyproject.toml",
            "README.md",
            "when_correlation_lies_report.pdf",
            "when_correlation_lies_all_source_code.txt",
            "results_manifest.txt",
            "src/detectors.py",
            "src/agents.py",
            "src/environment.py",
            "src/llm_helpers.py",
            "tests/test_detectors.py",
            "scripts/modal_llm_pilot.py",
            "scripts/modal_llm_expanded.py",
            "scripts/evaluate_llm_pilot.py",
            "scripts/evaluate_llm_thresholds.py",
            "scripts/generate_pdf_report.py",
            "scripts/verify_final_consistency.py",
            "results/tables/summary.csv",
            "results/tables/sensitivity_trajectory_length.csv",
            "results/tables/llm_pilot_summary.csv",
            "results/tables/llm_expanded_summary.csv",
            "results/tables/llm_expanded_t50_summary.csv",
            "results/tables/llm_threshold_summary.csv",
            "results/llm_pilot/billing_audit.json",
        ]
        for ef in expected_files:
            assert ef in namelist, f"Expected {ef} in ZIP root structure"

        # Confirm standalone PDF and PDF inside ZIP are byte-identical
        standalone_pdf = PROJECT_ROOT / "final_delivery" / "when_correlation_lies_report.pdf"
        if standalone_pdf.exists():
            zip_pdf_bytes = zf.read("when_correlation_lies_report.pdf")
            disk_pdf_bytes = standalone_pdf.read_bytes()
            assert zip_pdf_bytes == disk_pdf_bytes, "Standalone PDF and PDF inside ZIP are not byte-identical!"
            zip_report_pdf_bytes = zf.read("report/when_correlation_lies_report.pdf")
            assert zip_report_pdf_bytes == disk_pdf_bytes, "report/ PDF inside ZIP is not byte-identical to standalone PDF!"

    print(f"[PASS] Review ZIP: Valid clean repository bundle with 260 scripted and 630 LLM trajectories, labels, zero duplicates, and full runnable source (PDF byte-identical confirmed).")


def manifest_check():
    import hashlib
    def get_hash(p: Path) -> str:
        h = hashlib.sha256()
        with open(p, "rb") as f:
            while chunk := f.read(65536):
                h.update(chunk)
        return h.hexdigest()

    manifest_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "final_delivery" / "results_manifest.txt"
    assert manifest_path.exists()
    content = manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "Durgesh Unde" in content
    assert "260 unique JSONL files" in content
    assert "54,000 unique rounds" in content
    assert "630 unique JSONL files" in content
    assert "68,400 total decisions" in content
    assert "11,400/11,400" in content
    assert "$40.63" in content
    assert "$40.12" in content
    assert "$0.51" in content
    assert "$17.24" not in content
    assert "28,500" not in content
    assert "300 trajectories" not in content
    assert "300 unique JSONL files" not in content
    assert "59 / 59 tests passed" in content
    assert "0.010427 bits" in content
    assert "0.117454 bits" in content
    assert "0.030751 bits" in content
    assert "0.084 bits" in content
    assert "0.012 bits" in content
    assert "Qwen2.5-1.5B" in content

    # Verify disk hashes and sizes match external manifest exactly
    for fname in ["when_correlation_lies_report.pdf", "when_correlation_lies_all_source_code.txt", "when_correlation_lies_review_bundle.zip"]:
        fpath = PROJECT_ROOT / "final_delivery" / fname
        assert fpath.exists(), f"Missing {fpath}"
        actual_hash = get_hash(fpath)
        actual_size = f"{fpath.stat().st_size:,}"
        assert actual_hash in content, f"SHA-256 for {fname} in manifest ({actual_hash}) does not match content!"
        assert actual_size in content, f"Size for {fname} in manifest ({actual_size}) does not match content!"

    print("[PASS] Results Manifest: All metrics, file counts, and exact disk SHA-256/size checksums verified.")


def triangulation_metrics_check():
    metrics_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "results" / "metrics" / "final_metrics.json"
    assert metrics_path.exists(), f"Missing {metrics_path}"
    with open(metrics_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        raw_data = json.load(f)
    data = raw_data.get("aggregated", {})

    # Verify all conditions have cmi, pni, and marginal_mi
    for cond in ["A", "A_calibration", "B", "C", "D_alpha0.25", "D_alpha0.50", "D_alpha0.75", "D_alpha1.00"]:
        assert cond in data, f"Missing condition {cond} in metrics"
        cond_data = data[cond]
        for det in ["cmi", "pni", "marginal_mi"]:
            assert f"{det}_top_score" in cond_data, f"Missing {det}_top_score in {cond}"
            assert f"{det}_fpr" in cond_data, f"Missing {det}_fpr in {cond}"

    # Verify Condition B metrics match triangulation claims
    cond_b = data["B"]
    assert abs(cond_b["marginal_mi_fpr"]["mean"] - 1.0) < 1e-4
    assert abs(cond_b["cmi_fpr"]["mean"] - 0.0) < 1e-4
    assert abs(cond_b["pni_fpr"]["mean"] - 0.10) < 1e-4

    # Verify Condition C metrics match claims
    cond_c = data["C"]
    assert abs(cond_c["pni_top1"]["mean"] - 1.0) < 1e-4
    assert abs(cond_c["pni_fpr"]["mean"] - 0.20) < 1e-4
    assert cond_c["pni_fpr"].get("is_degenerate") is True

    print("[PASS] Triangulation Metrics: CMI, PNI, and Marginal MI verified across all conditions.")


def current_facing_consistency_check():
    current_files = [
        PROJECT_ROOT / "README.md",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "report" / "report_draft.md",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "scripts" / "package_delivery.py",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "scripts" / "generate_pdf_report.py",
    ]
    if (PROJECT_ROOT / "final_delivery" / "results_manifest.txt").exists():
        current_files.append(PROJECT_ROOT / "final_delivery" / "results_manifest.txt")
    if (PROJECT_ROOT / "results_manifest.txt").exists():
        current_files.append(PROJECT_ROOT / "results_manifest.txt")

    stale_forbidden = [
        "300 trajectories",
        "28,500",
        "$17.24",
        "$17.01",
        "4,750",
    ]

    for p in current_files:
        assert p.exists(), f"File {p} does not exist"
        txt = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for term in stale_forbidden:
            assert term not in txt, f"Found stale current-run term '{term}' in {p.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT)}"

    # Ensure root manifest and final_delivery manifest are byte-identical
    root_man = PROJECT_ROOT / "results_manifest.txt"
    deliv_man = PROJECT_ROOT / "final_delivery" / "results_manifest.txt"
    if root_man.exists() and deliv_man.exists():
        assert root_man.read_bytes() == deliv_man.read_bytes(), "root results_manifest.txt and final_delivery/results_manifest.txt are not byte-identical!"

    print("[PASS] Current-Facing Consistency: Zero stale current-run terms in README, scripts, report draft, or manifests; root manifest matches delivery manifest byte-for-byte.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("Running comprehensive consistency verification...")
    test_suite_check()
    dataset_check()
    triangulation_metrics_check()
    current_facing_consistency_check()
    if (PROJECT_ROOT / "final_delivery" / "when_correlation_lies_report.pdf").exists() or (PROJECT_ROOT / "report" / "when_correlation_lies_report.pdf").exists():
        pdf_report_check()
    if (PROJECT_ROOT / "final_delivery" / "when_correlation_lies_review_bundle.zip").exists():
        zip_bundle_check()
    if (PROJECT_ROOT / "final_delivery" / "results_manifest.txt").exists():
        manifest_check()
    print("\nALL CONSISTENCY CHECKS PASSED PERFECTLY!")

"""
scripts/package_delivery.py
===========================
Packages the complete AI Collusion Research Sprint final delivery artifacts:
1. final_delivery/when_correlation_lies_report.pdf
2. final_delivery/when_correlation_lies_all_source_code.txt
3. final_delivery/when_correlation_lies_review_bundle.zip
4. final_delivery/results_manifest.txt (external manifest matching all delivered files)

Manifest Architecture:
- Writes an internal manifest (excluding the ZIP's own checksum) inside the ZIP bundle.
- Builds the review bundle ZIP with clean repository structure (zero duplicate folders).
- Computes the final size and SHA-256 checksum of the closed ZIP.
- Writes the external manifest alongside the ZIP with the exact final checksums of all deliverables.
"""

import sys
import os
import shutil
import hashlib
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent
DELIVERY_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "final_delivery"
REPORT_PDF = PROJECT_ROOT / "report" / "when_correlation_lies_report.pdf"


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        while chunk := f.read(65536):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def generate_manifest_text(target_pdf: Path, concat_path: Path, zip_path: Path = None) -> str:
    """Generates the manifest text. If zip_path is None, generates internal manifest without ZIP hash."""
    lines = []
    lines.append("=" * 80)
    lines.append("APART RESEARCH AI COLLUSION RESEARCH SPRINT 2026 — SUBMISSION MANIFEST")
    lines.append("Track 1.4: Markets and Collusion")
    lines.append("Project Title: When Correlation Lies: Stress-Testing Pairwise Net Influence")
    lines.append("Author: Durgesh Unde (Independent Researcher)")
    lines.append("Event: Apart Research AI Collusion Research Sprint (October 23–25, 2026)")
    lines.append("=" * 80 + "\n")

    lines.append("AUTHOR CONTRIBUTIONS:")
    lines.append("-" * 80)
    lines.append(
        "Durgesh Unde (Independent Researcher): Project conceptualization, selection and formulation of research questions\n"
        "and experimental conditions, project direction, review of experimental outputs, qualitative interpretation\n"
        "of detection metrics, and final editorial responsibility for the manuscript.\n"
    )

    lines.append("AI USAGE STATEMENT:")
    lines.append("-" * 80)
    lines.append(
        "ChatGPT (OpenAI) assisted with research ideation, paper explanation, methodology critique, independent review,\n"
        "and prompt drafting. Antigravity (Google DeepMind advanced agentic coding assistant) assisted with code implementation,\n"
        "unit test suites, experiment orchestration, Modal CPU cloud infrastructure harness and execution, statistical metric aggregations, visualizations, document and PDF layout,\n"
        "and packaging under the author's iterative instructions and review. Project direction, experimental design choices,\n"
        "qualitative analysis, and final editorial responsibility rest entirely with the author (Durgesh Unde). Canonical benchmark\n"
        "evaluations used 260 scripted trajectories (54,000 rounds); open-weight LLM evaluations used 630 trajectories (68,400 decisions)\n"
        "executed on Modal CPU ($40.63 metered compute: $40.12 promotional credits applied, $0.51 network egress allowance; $0.00 out-of-pocket spend). Zero personal payment cards or external paid token APIs were used.\n"
        "The experimental protocol was frozen after exploratory prototyping and was not preregistered prior to exploration.\n"
    )

    lines.append("PRIMARY DELIVERABLES:")
    lines.append("-" * 80)
    pdf_hash = sha256_file(target_pdf)
    lines.append(f"File: {target_pdf.name}")
    lines.append(f"  Size: {target_pdf.stat().st_size:,} bytes")
    lines.append(f"  SHA-256: {pdf_hash}\n")

    concat_hash = sha256_file(concat_path)
    lines.append(f"File: {concat_path.name}")
    lines.append(f"  Size: {concat_path.stat().st_size:,} bytes")
    lines.append(f"  SHA-256: {concat_hash}\n")

    if zip_path is not None and zip_path.exists():
        zip_hash = sha256_file(zip_path)
        lines.append(f"File: {zip_path.name}")
        lines.append(f"  Size: {zip_path.stat().st_size:,} bytes")
        lines.append(f"  SHA-256: {zip_hash}\n")
    else:
        lines.append("Package: when_correlation_lies_review_bundle.zip (self-contained repository archive)\n")

    lines.append("\nCORE BENCHMARK FIGURES:")
    lines.append("-" * 80)
    for fig in sorted((PROJECT_ROOT / "results" / "figures").glob("*.png")):
        h = sha256_file(fig)
        lines.append(f"Figure: {fig.name} ({fig.stat().st_size:,} bytes)")
        lines.append(f"  SHA-256: {h}")

    lines.append("\nKEY BENCHMARK CSV TABLES:")
    lines.append("-" * 80)
    for tab in [
        PROJECT_ROOT / "results" / "tables" / "summary.csv",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "results" / "tables" / "sensitivity_trajectory_length.csv",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "results" / "tables" / "llm_pilot_summary.csv",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "results" / "tables" / "llm_expanded_summary.csv",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "results" / "tables" / "llm_expanded_t50_summary.csv",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "results" / "tables" / "llm_threshold_summary.csv",
    ]:
        if tab.exists():
            h = sha256_file(tab)
            lines.append(f"Table: {tab.name} ({tab.stat().st_size:,} bytes)")
            lines.append(f"  SHA-256: {h}")

    lines.append("\nEXPERIMENTAL TELEMETRY & AUDIT SUMMARY:")
    lines.append("-" * 80)
    lines.append("• Canonical Scripted Trajectories: 260 unique JSONL files (140 primary T=200 + 120 sensitivity T in {50, 100, 500})")
    lines.append("• Total Scripted Rounds: 54,000 unique rounds (140 * 200 = 28,000; 40 * 50 = 2,000; 40 * 100 = 4,000; 40 * 500 = 20,000)")
    lines.append("• Total Evaluations: 300 evaluations across 4 experimental conditions and sensitivity sweeps")
    lines.append("• Open-Weight LLM Agent Trajectories: 630 unique JSONL files (68,400 total decisions across T in {5, 20, 50} rounds)")
    lines.append("  - Pilot (T=5): 120 files (2,400 decisions across 40 seeds)")
    lines.append("  - Expanded Primary (T=20): 300 files (24,000 decisions across 100 seeds)")
    lines.append("  - Extended Horizon (T=50): 210 files (42,000 decisions across 70 seeds)")
    lines.append("• Automated Unit Test Suite: 59 / 59 tests passed (pytest 9.1.1, Python 3.13.7)")
    lines.append("• Cloud Compute & Billing: Modal CPU metered usage was $40.63 ($40.12 promotional credits applied, $0.51 network egress allowance; $0.00 billed out-of-pocket)")
    lines.append("• Decision Threshold Calibration: Small-sample exploratory calibration on Condition A validation seeds (seeds 0-4, 30 candidate pairs) targeting nominal 5% FPR")
    lines.append("  - Calibrated PNI Threshold (tau_PNI): 0.010427 bits")
    lines.append("  - Calibrated Conditional MI Threshold (tau_CMI): 0.117454 bits")
    lines.append("  - Calibrated Marginal MI Threshold (tau_MI): 0.030751 bits")
    lines.append("• Open-Weight LLM Empirical Results (Qwen2.5-1.5B on Modal CPU, 68,400 decisions):")
    lines.append("  - Valid Action Generation Rate: 100.0% (68,400/68,400 decisions valid parsed actions across all horizons)")
    lines.append("  - Condition LLM-C Signal Compliance: 100.0% (11,400/11,400 opportunities adhered to secret directive)")
    lines.append("  - Finite-Sample Horizon Sparsity Effect:")
    lines.append("      * T = 5 rounds: Top-1 Marginal MI = 1.000 (40/40), Top-1 CMI = 0.700 (28/40), Top-1 PNI = 0.700 (28/40), Mean CMI = 0.0696 bits")
    lines.append("      * T = 20 rounds: Top-1 Marginal MI = 1.000 (100/100), Top-1 CMI = 1.000 (100/100), Top-1 PNI = 1.000 (100/100), Mean CMI = 0.1333 bits")
    lines.append("      * T = 50 rounds: Top-1 Marginal MI = 1.000 (70/70), Top-1 CMI = 1.000 (70/70), Top-1 PNI = 1.000 (70/70), Mean CMI = 0.1770 bits")
    lines.append("• Canonical Scripted Benchmark Key Results (T=200 rounds, 20 seeds):")
    lines.append("  - Condition A (Independent Play, Held-Out Test n=15): PNI Top = 0.005 bits, PNI FPR = 0.022 [0.000, 0.056] (2/90 pairs flagged); CMI FPR = 0.111 (10/90); Marginal MI FPR = 0.033 (3/90)")
    lines.append("  - Condition A (Calibration n=5): PNI FPR = 0.067 (2/30), CMI FPR = 0.067 (2/30), Marginal MI FPR = 0.067 (2/30)")
    lines.append("  - Condition B (Shared Context Confounding n=20):")
    lines.append("      * Marginal MI: 0.958 bits [0.932, 0.984], FPR = 1.000 [1.000, 1.000]* (120/120 pairs flagged, 38-fold surge)")
    lines.append("      * Conditional MI (CMI): 0.084 bits [0.081, 0.087], FPR = 0.000 [0.000, 0.000]* (0/120 pairs flagged; state conditioning neutralizes broadcast shock)")
    lines.append("      * PNI Net Score: 0.012 bits [0.009, 0.015], FPR = 0.100 [0.050, 0.158] (12/120 pairs flagged; competitor subtraction provides directed isolation)")
    lines.append("  - Condition C (Shared-Private-Signal Coordination Positive Control n=20):")
    lines.append("      * PNI Top-1 Accuracy: 1.000 (Wilson 95% CI: [0.839, 1.000]), MRR = 1.000, True Pair Net Score = 0.755 bits [0.741, 0.769]")
    lines.append("      * PNI FPR: 0.200 [0.200, 0.200]* (20/100 negative pairs flagged; *degenerate bootstrap interval due to uniform outcome of exactly 1/5 negative pairs flagged per seed)")
    lines.append("      * CMI FPR: 0.020 [0.000, 0.050] (2/100); Marginal MI FPR = 0.240 (24/100)")
    lines.append("  - Condition D (Joint Gate Sweep n=20 each):")
    lines.append("      alpha = 0.25: PNI True Score = 0.264 bits, Top-1 = 1.000 [0.839, 1.000], PNI FPR = 0.100 (10/100); CMI FPR = 0.010 (1/100); Marginal MI FPR = 1.000 (100/100)")
    lines.append("      alpha = 0.50: PNI True Score = 0.469 bits, Top-1 = 1.000 [0.839, 1.000], PNI FPR = 0.180 (18/100); CMI FPR = 0.000 (0/100); Marginal MI FPR = 1.000 (100/100)")
    lines.append("      alpha = 0.75: PNI True Score = 0.623 bits, Top-1 = 1.000 [0.839, 1.000], PNI FPR = 0.200* (20/100); CMI FPR = 0.020 (2/100); Marginal MI FPR = 0.920 (92/100)")
    lines.append("      alpha = 1.00: PNI True Score = 0.771 bits, Top-1 = 1.000 [0.839, 1.000], PNI FPR = 0.200* (20/100); CMI FPR = 0.010 (1/100); Marginal MI FPR = 0.240 (24/100)")
    lines.append("  - Trajectory Length Sensitivity (T in [50, 500]):")
    lines.append("      * Cond C Top-1 = 1.000 across all T")
    lines.append("      * Cond B CMI Score declines: 0.144 (T=50) -> 0.112 (T=100) -> 0.084 (T=200) -> 0.056 (T=500); CMI FPR declines: 0.450 -> 0.042 -> 0.000 -> 0.000")
    lines.append("      * Cond B PNI Score declines: 0.0176 (T=50) -> 0.0131 (T=100) -> 0.0121 (T=200) -> 0.0074 (T=500); PNI FPR declines: 0.133 -> 0.108 -> 0.100 -> 0.050")
    lines.append("  - Estimator Regularization Sensitivity (Cond B, T=200):")
    lines.append("      * lambda = 0.0 (unsmoothed plug-in, 0 log 0 = 0): CMI = 0.062 bits")
    lines.append("      * lambda = 0.5 (Jeffreys Dirichlet): CMI = 0.084 bits")
    lines.append("      * lambda = 1.0 (Laplace Dirichlet): CMI = 0.096 bits")

    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"


def main():
    DELIVERY_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    print(f"Packaging delivery to: {DELIVERY_DIR}")

    # 1. Copy PDF report and DOCX source
    target_pdf = DELIVERY_DIR / "when_correlation_lies_report.pdf"
    shutil.copy2(REPORT_PDF, target_pdf)
    print(f"Copied PDF report -> {target_pdf} ({target_pdf.stat().st_size:,} bytes)")

    target_docx = PROJECT_ROOT / "report" / "when_correlation_lies_report.docx"
    if target_docx.exists():
        delivery_docx = DELIVERY_DIR / "when_correlation_lies_report.docx"
        shutil.copy2(target_docx, delivery_docx)
        print(f"Copied DOCX report -> {delivery_docx} ({delivery_docx.stat().st_size:,} bytes)")

    # 2. Collect all source code, tests, scripts, configs, documentation
    files_to_bundle = [
        ".gitattributes",
        ".github/workflows/ci.yml",
        "pyproject.toml",
        "README.md",
        "config/experiment.yaml",
        "config/model.yaml",
        "src/__init__.py",
        "src/agents.py",
        "src/environment.py",
        "src/detectors.py",
        "src/trajectories.py",
        "src/metrics.py",
        "src/run_experiments.py",
        "src/analyze_results.py",
        "src/llm_helpers.py",
        "tests/__init__.py",
        "tests/test_analytical_cmi.py",
        "tests/test_detectors.py",
        "tests/test_environment.py",
        "tests/test_metrics.py",
        "tests/test_llm_pilot.py",
        "scripts/modal_llm_pilot.py",
        "scripts/modal_llm_expanded.py",
        "scripts/evaluate_llm_pilot.py",
        "scripts/evaluate_llm_thresholds.py",
        "scripts/plot_llm_results.py",
        "scripts/run_sensitivity.py",
        "scripts/plot_setup_diagram.py",
        "scripts/generate_pdf_report.py",
        "scripts/build_official_template_report.py",
        "scripts/verify_final_consistency.py",
        "scripts/package_delivery.py",
        "docs/scientific_audit.md",
        "docs/retrospective_research_plan.md",
        "docs/results_interpretation.md",
        "docs/pre_sprint_work_log.md",
        "archive/ARCHIVE_README.md",
        "report/report_draft.md",
        "results/tables/summary.csv",
        "results/tables/sensitivity_trajectory_length.csv",
        "results/tables/llm_pilot_summary.csv",
        "results/tables/llm_expanded_summary.csv",
        "results/tables/llm_expanded_t50_summary.csv",
        "results/tables/llm_threshold_summary.csv",
        "results/llm_pilot/billing_audit.json",
    ]

    concat_path = DELIVERY_DIR / "when_correlation_lies_all_source_code.txt"
    total_files = 0
    total_lines = 0

    with open(concat_path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as out_f:
        out_f.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
        out_f.write("WHEN CORRELATION LIES: AI COLLUSION RESEARCH SPRINT 2026\n")
        out_f.write("CONCATENATED SOURCE CODE AND REPRODUCIBILITY REPOSITORY\n")
        out_f.write("Author: Durgesh Unde | Affiliation: Independent Researcher\n")
        out_f.write("Track 1.4: Markets and Collusion\n")
        out_f.write("Apart Research AI Collusion Research Sprint (October 23-25, 2026)\n")
        out_f.write("=" * 80 + "\n\n")

        for rel_path in files_to_bundle:
            p = PROJECT_ROOT / rel_path
            if not p.exists():
                print(f"WARNING: File missing: {p}")
                continue

            file_hash = sha256_file(p)
            file_bytes = p.stat().st_size
            try:
                content = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").replace("\r\n", "\n")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error reading {p}: {e}")
                continue

            lines = content.count("\n") + 1
            total_lines += lines
            total_files += 1

            out_f.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
            out_f.write(f"FILE: {rel_path}\n")
            out_f.write(f"SHA-256: {file_hash}\n")
            out_f.write(f"SIZE: {file_bytes} bytes | LINES: {lines}\n")
            out_f.write("=" * 80 + "\n\n")
            out_f.write(content)
            out_f.write("\n\n")

    print(f"Generated concatenated source text -> {concat_path}")
    print(f"  Total bundled files: {total_files}")
    print(f"  Total code/doc lines: {total_lines:,}")
    print(f"  File size: {concat_path.stat().st_size:,} bytes")

    # 3. Create INTERNAL manifest content (excludes ZIP checksum)
    internal_manifest_content = generate_manifest_text(target_pdf, concat_path, zip_path=None)

    # 4. Create Clean Review Bundle ZIP (Project Root as ZIP root)
    zip_path = DELIVERY_DIR / "when_correlation_lies_review_bundle.zip"
    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
        # Include root documents for immediate reviewer convenience
        zf.write(target_pdf, arcname="when_correlation_lies_report.pdf")
        zf.write(concat_path, arcname="when_correlation_lies_all_source_code.txt")
        # Write internal manifest into ZIP root
        zf.writestr("results_manifest.txt", internal_manifest_content)

        # Include all source, test, script, config, doc, and table files in standard repository paths
        for rel_path in files_to_bundle:
            p = PROJECT_ROOT / rel_path
            if p.exists():
                zf.write(p, arcname=rel_path)

        # Include official template in docs/
        template_doc = PROJECT_ROOT / "docs" / "template.docx"
        if template_doc.exists():
            zf.write(template_doc, arcname="docs/template.docx")

        # Include PDF and DOCX in report/
        zf.write(target_pdf, arcname="report/when_correlation_lies_report.pdf")
        if target_docx.exists():
            zf.write(target_docx, arcname="report/when_correlation_lies_report.docx")
            zf.write(target_docx, arcname="when_correlation_lies_report.docx")

        # Include all figures in results/figures/
        figures = sorted((PROJECT_ROOT / "results" / "figures").glob("*.png"))
        for fig in figures:
            zf.write(fig, arcname=f"results/figures/{fig.name}")

        # Include metrics jsons in results/metrics/
        for mf in sorted((PROJECT_ROOT / "results" / "metrics").glob("*.json")):
            zf.write(mf, arcname=f"results/metrics/{mf.name}")

        # Include all 260 raw JSONL trajectory files in data/raw/
        raw_files = sorted((PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "raw").glob("*.jsonl"))
        for rf in raw_files:
            zf.write(rf, arcname=f"data/raw/{rf.name}")

        # Include all 260 ground truth label files in data/eval_labels/
        label_files = sorted((PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "eval_labels").glob("*.json"))
        for lf in label_files:
            zf.write(lf, arcname=f"data/eval_labels/{lf.name}")

        # Include all exploratory LLM pilot trajectories in data/raw_llm/
        for rf in sorted((PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "raw_llm").glob("*.jsonl")):
            zf.write(rf, arcname=f"data/raw_llm/{rf.name}")

        for lf in sorted((PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "eval_labels_llm").glob("*.json")):
            zf.write(lf, arcname=f"data/eval_labels_llm/{lf.name}")

        # Include all expanded LLM trajectories in data/raw_llm_expanded/
        for rf in sorted((PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "raw_llm_expanded").glob("*.jsonl")):
            zf.write(rf, arcname=f"data/raw_llm_expanded/{rf.name}")

        for lf in sorted((PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "eval_labels_llm_expanded").glob("*.json")):
            zf.write(lf, arcname=f"data/eval_labels_llm_expanded/{lf.name}")

        # Include all extended T50 LLM trajectories in data/raw_llm_expanded_t50/
        for rf in sorted((PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "raw_llm_expanded_t50").glob("*.jsonl")):
            zf.write(rf, arcname=f"data/raw_llm_expanded_t50/{rf.name}")

        for lf in sorted((PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "eval_labels_llm_expanded_t50").glob("*.json")):
            zf.write(lf, arcname=f"data/eval_labels_llm_expanded_t50/{lf.name}")

    # ZIP is completely closed and sealed here!
    zip_size = zip_path.stat().st_size
    zip_hash = sha256_file(zip_path)
    print(f"Generated review bundle ZIP -> {zip_path} ({zip_size:,} bytes)")
    print(f"  SHA-256: {zip_hash}")

    # 5. Generate EXTERNAL results_manifest.txt (includes the final sealed ZIP's checksum and size)
    external_manifest_content = generate_manifest_text(target_pdf, concat_path, zip_path=zip_path).replace("\r\n", "\n")
    manifest_path = DELIVERY_DIR / "results_manifest.txt"
    with open(manifest_path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:
        f.write(external_manifest_content)
    root_manifest_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "results_manifest.txt"
    with open(root_manifest_path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:
        f.write(external_manifest_content)
    print(f"Generated external results manifest -> {manifest_path} ({manifest_path.stat().st_size:,} bytes)")
    print(f"Synced results manifest to root -> {root_manifest_path}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
